#### AUTH-016 — Layered login attempt throttling

- **Review part:** 01
- **User roles:** Unauthenticated login actors; all accounts protected.
- **User objective:** Bound credential-guessing attempts without creating account-enumeration responses.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Before DAO authentication, identity resolver maps username to account ID or normalized unknown identity; LoginRateLimiter consumes IP, identity and IP+identity token buckets; when limited, controller returns generic 429 and capped Retry-After; on success account/pair buckets reset (IP history retained).
- **Frontend / Android:** [web/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx) shows backend error; Android [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/auth/interfaces/ui/LoginViewModel.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/auth/interfaces/ui/LoginViewModel.kt) displays login failure. Browser doesn't implement a separate lockout countdown.
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/app/api/v1/auth/login/route.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/api/v1/auth/login/route.ts) preserves upstream error statuses/headers; [web/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts) forwards Retry-After in selected headers.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/application/LoginAuthenticationService.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/application/LoginAuthenticationService.java), [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/application/LoginRateLimiter.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/application/LoginRateLimiter.java), [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/application/LoginRateLimitIdentityResolver.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/application/LoginRateLimitIdentityResolver.java), [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/config/LoginRateLimitProperties.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/config/LoginRateLimitProperties.java).
- **Database / ledger:** In-memory bounded Caffeine counters, not persisted across process restarts or shared between instances.
- **External dependencies:** None.
- **Authorization:** Public login admission protection; default enabled and configurable through application.yml.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** 429 generic message + Retry-After, or 503 when limiter fails; credential response remains generic 401. No durable account-lock record.

### Part 02 — Customer KYC and onboarding

**Status:** COMPLETE (source-based; not certification of live integrated-stack acceptance).

**Reconfirmed main SHA snapshots on 2026-11-23:** bank-web 012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27; bank-core 9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3; bank-service cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3; bank-app 9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38. All four recursive trees were non-truncated. Only this documentation branch is written.

**Complete intended web journey:** verified login identity (AUTH-001/002/003) -> authenticated CUSTOMER -> detect missing banking Customer / KYC -> four independent uploads (ID front, ID back, selfie, proof of address) -> consent and JSON case submit -> Spring PENDING case -> FastAPI aggregate checks or Admin review -> latest case APPROVED -> separate customer-details PUT /api/v1/customers/me -> distinct Customer UUID linked to current User UUID -> self-service account opening eligible. Approval alone never creates the banking Customer record.

**State machine:** Declared enum transitions are distinguished from reachability through actual controller and frontend paths.

| Status | Entered by | Allowed outgoing states in code | Customer outcome |
|---|---|---|---|
| PENDING | Created by Core after four owned uploads+consent | AUTO_REVIEW, UNDER_REVIEW, NEEDS_MORE_INFO | Queued; no duplicate active case |
| AUTO_REVIEW | Core orchestration | UNDER_REVIEW, NEEDS_MORE_INFO, APPROVED, REJECTED | FastAPI checks; auto approval/rejection/referral |
| UNDER_REVIEW | ML referral/error or manual decision | APPROVED, REJECTED, NEEDS_MORE_INFO | Human decision required; user sees reason/checks |
| NEEDS_MORE_INFO | ADMIN decision | UNDER_REVIEW, REJECTED allowed by enum | User sees reason but has no connected correction/resubmit route |
| APPROVED | Mapped FastAPI APPROVE or ADMIN decision | None; terminal | Enables separate Customer upsert, does not create one |
| REJECTED | Mapped FastAPI REJECT or ADMIN decision | None; terminal | Web may start new case with new uploads |
| SUPERSEDED | Enum declared; no explicit source-confirmed transition into status | None; terminal | Web restart offered but entry state unproven |

**Upload and persistence architecture:** Web 1 MiB image limit; Spring /kyc/upload accepts up to 10 MiB and 20 uploads/user UTC day, while web KYC BFF caps inbound body at 11 MiB. Core filesystem holds bytes and MySQL kyc_uploads metadata (hash, path, userId), kyc_cases, kyc_checks, kyc_idem_keys. FastAPI does not store uploaded images or create Customers. Uploaded images are separate persistent steps before case commit; incomplete browser drafts are not persisted.

**ML scope and quality distinction:** [service/app/api/v1/kyc.py](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-service/blob/cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3/app/api/v1/kyc.py) and [service/app/features/factory.py](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-service/blob/cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3/app/features/factory.py) implement default simple/heuristic image assessments and optional model-backed implementations. [service/app/core/policy.py](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-service/blob/cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3/app/core/policy.py) uses FACE_MATCH, LIVENESS, OCR_ID, DOC_CLASS to decide APPROVE/REJECT/UNDER_REVIEW; ADDRESS_MATCH and DOC_VALIDITY are computed but not currently used for its decision. Source-based automatic APPROVE is not proof of real-world identity assurance. GitHub models directory contains a dictionary but no deployable advanced ONNX weight files; [service/models/README.md](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-service/blob/cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3/models/README.md) references an external snapshot not inspected. Model inference was not executed.

**Client differences:** Web uses a manual status refresh, recoverable uncertain submission and rejected-case restart; Android has CameraX/ML Kit capture assistance, direct Retrofit uploads, status polling every ~3 seconds and approved-customer registration. Android assigns placeholder local liveness/face-match values 0.9 for picked selfie; those values are not FastAPI check results. Android has no matching idempotent submission recovery, RFI correction or rejected-case reapplication workflow. No local draft restore established.

**Admin and downstream boundaries:** Admin role-specific decision/change-of-status is reviewed only as lifecycle dependency; full queue review remains Part 10. Self-service account-opening eligibility is confirmed in Spring and web, but account lifecycle/ledger remains Part 04, staff creation remains Part 07, Android-wide review Part 14, cross-cutting operational details Part 15.

**Evidence:** Existing web Playwright suite runs [web/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs) rather than live bank-core. Spring [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/integration/KycMlContractIT.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/integration/KycMlContractIT.java) can send environment-gated fixtures to FastAPI, but no test run evidence was accessible or executed. No F1-F8 attributable real-stack artifacts found in four GitHub trees. All confidence labels are Source-confirmed and no Level 5 scores are claimed.



#### KYC-001 — Authenticated KYC entry and eligibility discovery
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Signed-in web CUSTOMER, backend authenticated identity.

Onboarding retrieves GET /kyc/me and /customers/me; missing case 404 becomes an empty document form, existing case becomes status and checks, approved case enables Customer details, rejected case offers restart. No Customer is generated merely by registration.

#### KYC-002 — Four-document upload and Spring file persistence
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Authenticated KYC applicant.
- **User objective:** Persist four distinct images before application submission.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Web selects ID front, ID back, selfie and address proof separately; local validation accepts JPEG/PNG/WebP, nonempty files at most 1 MiB. Each multipart POST /api/kyc/upload sends file and type through CSRF-protected KYC BFF. Spring requires valid type/content-type, allows up to 10 MiB, limits 20 uploads per user UTC day, stores bytes under date and UUID file name, calculates SHA-256 and creates kyc_uploads metadata; returns UUID used in later case submission.
- **Frontend / Android:** [web/components/banking/KycDocumentUpload.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/KycDocumentUpload.tsx); [web/components/banking/CustomerOnboarding.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerOnboarding.tsx)

#### KYC-003 — Consent-based case submission and duplicate handling
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Authenticated user who owns four uploads.
- **User objective:** Create an authoritative KYC application without accidental repeated submissions.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Web confirms four distinct upload IDs and consent, POSTs JSON docFrontId/docBackId/selfieId/addressId/consent plus X-Idempotency-Key to /api/kyc/submit. Spring checks consent, same-user ownership, distinct UUIDs and matching document types, creates KycCase PENDING then invokes auto-review. If the latest case is PENDING/AUTO_REVIEW/UNDER_REVIEW/NEEDS_MORE_INFO, controller returns existing status/caseId without creating a duplicate. On ambiguous browser timeout, frontend GETs current case before deliberate retry with same key and payload.
- **Frontend / Android:** [web/components/banking/CustomerOnboarding.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerOnboarding.tsx)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/kyc.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/kyc.ts); [web/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts)
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycCaseController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycCaseController.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/application/impl/KycCaseServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/application/impl/KycCaseServiceImpl.java)
- **Database / ledger:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycCase.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycCase.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycIdemKey.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycIdemKey.java); [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql)
- **External dependencies:** After case persistence Spring initiates KYC-004 FastAPI aggregate assessment.
- **Authorization:** Authenticated principal determines case owner; all 4 upload UUIDs must belong to same principal and match corresponding types.

#### KYC-004 — Automated FastAPI assessment and Spring decision mapping
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Spring internal KYC automation and FastAPI.
- **User objective:** Evaluate submitted images and persist assessment and case decision.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Spring on submission or scheduled PENDING batch marks AUTO_REVIEW, reads 4 files, base64 encodes, sends POST /api/v1/kyc/aggregate with bankUserId and caseId through authenticated WebClient. FastAPI computes FACE_MATCH, LIVENESS, OCR_ID, DOC_CLASS, ADDRESS_MATCH and DOC_VALIDITY using configured factory backends and thresholds, returns decision/reasons/checks; Spring stores individual KycCheck rows and maps APPROVE to APPROVED, REJECT to REJECTED, others to UNDER_REVIEW. Absent/failed ML response triggers UNDER_REVIEW with ERROR check.

#### KYC-005 — Customer KYC status and check visibility
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Authenticated case owner on web.
- **User objective:** See persisted case state, reasons and automatic checks after navigating away.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Customer onboarding GET /api/kyc/me for latest by user UUID and GET /api/kyc/{caseId}/checks. Core returns caseId, status, IDs, time, reviewer reason and checks; web maps seven enumerated statuses to messages, shows decision reason, check pass/fail/scores, special ML-unavailable note, and offers manual Refresh. Case persists across visits unlike unsubmitted file draft.

#### KYC-006 — Admin KYC decision affecting customer status
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** ADMIN reviewer and customer awaiting outcome.
- **User objective:** Convert a reviewed case into approved, rejected or more-info state.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Admin reads GET /admin/kyc status queue, case checks and authenticated documents, confirms POST /admin/kyc/{id}/decision with APPROVED, REJECTED or NEEDS_MORE_INFO and reason. Core enforces ADMIN role and enum state transitions; writes reviewerId, decision reason/time and status. Customer sees changed latest KYC through GET /kyc/me; APPROVED allows customer /customers/me registration. Detailed Admin workspace evaluation remains Part 10.

#### KYC-007 — Rejected-case new verification application
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Web CUSTOMER with latest REJECTED case.
- **User objective:** Reapply using new documents instead of reopening a terminal case.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Web displays REJECTED reason and Start a new verification. It clears local draft and permits four new uploads, fresh consent and POST submit. Core REJECTED is terminal, but active-case filter excludes REJECTED so new KycCase row is created; GET /kyc/me then returns newest case. SUPERSEDED also exposes restart in web UI though no source-confirmed setter/transition into that enum state was identified.

- **Database / ledger:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycCase.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycCase.java) (prior case retained, new case created)

#### KYC-008 — Needs-more-information correction and resubmission
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Customer with NEEDS_MORE_INFO and Admin reviewer.
- **User objective:** Replace requested documents and resume review.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** ADMIN can mark NEEDS_MORE_INFO and customer sees reason. Enum allows transition to UNDER_REVIEW/REJECTED; KycCaseServiceImpl contains a service branch to replace document IDs in NEEDS_MORE_INFO. #### KYC-009 — Protected KYC document retrieval and Admin preview
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Document owner or ADMIN.
- **User objective:** View images already stored for KYC.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Admin selects uploaded document; browser GET /api/kyc/file/{uuid} through protected BFF, Spring verifies current user equals uploadedBy or ROLE_ADMIN then reads file bytes and returns image MIME and inline filename. BFF adds no-store, nosniff and sandbox response headers. Admin renders secure preview; missing/nonowner document receives error. No customer-facing document gallery is wired.
- **Frontend / Android:** [web/components/banking/AdminKycDocumentPreview.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/AdminKycDocumentPreview.tsx); [web/components/banking/AdminKycReview.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/AdminKycReview.tsx)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/kyc.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/kyc.ts); [web/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts)
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycFileController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycFileController.java)
- **Database / ledger:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/support/storage/FileSystemStorageServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/support/storage/FileSystemStorageServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycUpload.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycUpload.java)
- **External dependencies:** No FastAPI call; original bytes served from core disk.
- **Authorization:** Ownership or ADMIN checked server-side, with active JWT/session.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Binary file for permitted user, rejection to unauthorized, missing document or file can fail after retention cleanup.

#### KYC-010 — Scheduled KYC image retention cleanup
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Scheduled Spring background job.
- **User objective:** Remove files associated with older terminal cases.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Daily @Scheduled at 03:15 service scans KycCase rows; APPROVED and REJECTED cases updated at least 30 days ago have their four referenced file UUIDs removed via FileStorageService.delete, which deletes filesystem bytes and KycUpload DB metadata; case and check rows remain. Missing file cleanup is tolerated.
- **Frontend / Android:** Not applicable: background operation without customer UI.
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable: scheduled service, not an HTTP request.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/application/impl/KycRetentionService.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/application/impl/KycRetentionService.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/support/storage/FileSystemStorageServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/support/storage/FileSystemStorageServiceImpl.java)
- **Database / ledger:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycCase.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycCase.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycUpload.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/domain/KycUpload.java)
- **External dependencies:** No FastAPI involvement.
- **Authorization:** System-internal scheduled task, not a customer action.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Terminal old image bytes and metadata deleted, case status and checks remain.
- **Existing automated tests:** No dedicated retention-service test identified in inspected GitHub KYC test tree; not executed.
- **Available live verification:** No attributable real-stack four-service acceptance trace was accessible; tests inspected as code, not run during Part 02.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented**, scoped to this feature.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.

#### KYC-011 — Post-approval banking Customer self-registration/upsert
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Authenticated CUSTOMER with latest approved KYC.
- **User objective:** Create the banking Customer row distinct from User login account.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** APPROVED latest KYC reveals web CustomerRecordForm, prefilled from GET /users/me when available. Customer supplies first/last name, gender, email, phone, DOB, optional address, status. Browser PUT /api/v1/customers/me includes userId; Spring checks latest KYC APPROVED (else 409), overwrites submitted userId from principal, looks up Customer by owning User UUID and creates new Customer row or updates existing. Separate Customer UUID assigned and one-to-one association established; browser reloads GET /customers/me and displays Onboarding complete/Open account.
- **Frontend / Android:** [web/components/banking/CustomerRecordForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerRecordForm.tsx); [web/components/banking/CustomerOnboarding.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerOnboarding.tsx)

#### KYC-012 — Approved KYC and Customer gate for self-service account opening
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Authenticated CUSTOMER opening own account.
- **User objective:** Prevent self-service banking account creation before customer onboarding.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Web account-opening eligibility checks GET /customers/me and /kyc/me, shows Continue onboarding when record missing or latest case not APPROVED. Spring POST /api/v1/accounts requires CUSTOMER, looks up Customer linked to current User UUID, checks latest KycCase status APPROVED, otherwise rejects with missing-customer or ERR_KYC_NOT_APPROVED before account opening. Teller-assisted account opening follows different path reserved for Part 08; account/ledger details reserved Part 04.

#### KYC-013 — Android multi-step document capture and KYC submission
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Logged-in Android banking app user.
- **User objective:** Capture images, consent and submit KYC through direct Retrofit.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Android Verify Identity displays Document -> Selfie -> Address -> Review steps. Camera/gallery capture, ML Kit local face/blink processing and document quality helpers aid selection. Each selected URI asynchronously uploads multipart POST kyc/upload, IDs retained in ViewModel; four required IDs and consent trigger POST kyc/submit. On recognized response status app navigates to status page. Selfie selection/capture assigns local face/liveness scores of 0.9 as UI placeholders; these are not FastAPI results.
- **Frontend / Android:** [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/interfaces/ui/KycRoute.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/interfaces/ui/KycRoute.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/interfaces/ui/KycScreens.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/interfaces/ui/KycScreens.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/interfaces/ui/KycViewModel.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/interfaces/ui/KycViewModel.kt)

- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycUploadController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycUploadController.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycCaseController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycCaseController.java)
- **Database / ledger:** Same core filesystem and KycCase rows; mobile URI and upload-ID state only in app memory.
- **External dependencies:** Local ML Kit capture guidance; actual FastAPI approval separately performed by Spring after case submission.
- **Authorization:** Android Bearer from AUTH-011; backend checks owner and consent.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Native step UI/upload/submission; generic failed submission returns home; no documented safe retry reconciliation.
- **Existing automated tests:** No Android instrumented KYC acceptance test identified; backend/web mock tests do not establish device execution.
- **Available live verification:** No attributable real-stack four-service acceptance trace was accessible; tests inspected as code, not run during Part 02.

#### KYC-014 — Android KYC polling and approved customer registration
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Android user with active/reviewed case.
- **User objective:** See result and register Customer on approval.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** Android status screen polls GET kyc/me and GET kyc/{id}/checks about every 3 seconds until APPROVED/REJECTED/NEEDS_MORE_INFO. On APPROVED it shows Proceed to Customer Registration; form gathers name, gender, email, phone, DOB, optional address and status ACTIVE. Repository gets user UUID from profile and PUTs /api/v1/customers/me; Spring requires approved latest KYC and creates/upserts Customer. Successful mobile form navigates Home.

#### KYC-015 — Standalone FastAPI identity-check endpoints
- **Review part:** 02.
- **User roles:** Trusted internal FastAPI client, not ordinary banking customer.
- **User objective:** Call an individual face-match, liveness, OCR or document-classification operation.
- **Workflow boundary and complete steps:** FastAPI exposes POST /api/v1/kyc/face/match, /liveness, /ocr/id and /doc/class, accepting JSON base64 image fields with required per-operation inputs. Each calls selected backend factory and returns KycResult type, score, passed and detailsJson. Spring's customer KYC flow only calls /aggregate; standalone routes neither create KycCase nor change bank approval state.
- **Frontend / Android:** No web or Android UI for standalone internal endpoints.
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Internal FastAPI JSON POST with X-Bank-Core-Auth; no BFF or end-user JWT.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/integration/MlKycClient.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/integration/MlKycClient.java) invokes only aggregate in actual KYC workflow
- **Database / ledger:** No customer KYC DB writes by standalone FastAPI check calls.

### Part 03 — Customer dashboard and profile

**Status:** COMPLETE (source-confirmed; no real-stack acceptance).

**Source snapshot 2026-11-23:** bank-web `012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27`; bank-core `9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3`; bank-service `cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3`; bank-app `9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38`. All four main HEADs rechecked, matching Part 02. No FastAPI dashboard/profile dependency exists, so bank-service was not redundantly analyzed for this part.

**Scope:** This review covers dashboard/profile presentation, data loading, navigation, Customer/User field ownership, edit discoverability, local settings, Android parity and placeholder boundaries. AUTH-003/004/007/009/010/011/014 and KYC-001/005/011/012/014 are reused as dependencies; they are not counted again. Account opening Part 04; transaction/ledger Part 05; Internal Pay Part 06; staff Customer CRUD Part 07; entire Android app Part 14.

**Web architecture and information:** CUSTOMER roleHome -> /customer -> ProtectedLayout/AuthGuard. CustomerDashboard concurrently GETs /api/v1/customers/me (existence) and /api/v1/accounts/my (owner-scoped AccountResponseDTO list). Only if Customer exists and account list is empty does it GET /kyc/me to decide opening vs onboarding CTA. It displays username from AuthProvider, each Account's number/type/status and account.balance as a lossless decimal string with LKR suffix; no bank-wide total, chart, account count metric or recent feed. Shared header has Accounts/Deposit/Withdraw/Pay/Profile links, logo returns CUSTOMER home, dashboard Start transaction points to Deposit. Clicking links does not itself execute banking workflows.

**User versus Customer ownership and edit/display map:**

| Field group | Authoritative source | Read API | Presentation | Edit boundary |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| User username/name/role/email | User (users table) | GET /api/v1/users/me | Web Profile username/name/role and email; Android Profile also shows all | Web name/username read-only; email AUTH-009; Android edit User (username AUTH-014) |
| User contact/location | User address/city/state/country/postalCode and home/work/office/mobile numbers | GET /api/v1/users/me | Android Profile; omitted from Web Profile | Android PUT /users/me; web no general editor |
| Customer name/email/phone/address | Customer (customers table); duplicates can diverge from User | GET /api/v1/customers/me | Web onboarding form; Android Customer Registration, not either User Profile | PUT /customers/me after latest KYC approved, KYC-011 |
| Customer DOB/gender/status/UUID/timestamps | Customer, separate customerId and linked userId | GET /api/v1/customers/me | Available in DTO, form uses DOB/gender, dashboard checks record existence | Self-upsert DTO carries status; no dedicated /customer/profile editor |
| Account number/type/status/balance | Account (accounts table), not User/Customer fields | GET /api/v1/accounts/my | Web AccountSummary; Android My Accounts list, Home selected/first balance | Read-only display; account lifecycle Part 04 |
| KYC state | Latest KycCase.status | GET /kyc/me | Web conditional empty-account next step; Android My Accounts after Customer missing | KYC review/updates already Part 02 |

**Onboarding, loading, empty and unavailable states:**

| Customer situation | Web | Android |
|---|---|---|
| Missing Customer; no case or KYC pending | Web redirects to /customer/onboarding and that page exposes exact case status | Android Home no KYC; My Accounts on missing Customer shows Start Verification |
| Latest UNDER_REVIEW / NEEDS_MORE_INFO / REJECTED | Web onboarding shows actual case status; REJECTED may restart; RFI cannot correct (KYC-008) | Android My Accounts generic verification CTA, KYC Status separate |
| APPROVED KYC, no Customer | Web still redirects onboarding and shows CustomerRecordForm | Android My Accounts offers Register as Customer |
| Customer exists, zero accounts, APPROVED KYC | Web shows Open account CTA | Android My Accounts empty shows Open Account |
| Customer exists, zero accounts, not approved / status unknown | Web shows Continue/View onboarding | Android list path does not query KYC unless Customer missing |
| Customer with accounts | Web per-account cards and View onboarding; no KYC call at this point | Android Home preferred/first balance and real My Accounts list |
| CLOSED / FROZEN account among returned records | Web card shows accountStatus, not removed by dashboard | Android account row status shown; account lifecycle Part 04 |
| Accounts query error | Web error message; no inline retry or status lookup | Android My Accounts retry; Android Home silently shows zero |

**Web profile boundary:** /customer/profile uses GET /users/me and renders User.username/firstName/lastName/roleName/email only. Email/password controls were fully scoped in Part 01. The User DTO also contains home/work/office/mobile numbers and geographic fields but the web page does not show or edit them. For banking Customer fields the user instead revisits /customer/onboarding, where approved latest KYC enables CustomerRecordForm GET/PUT /customers/me; update persists Customer only and GET rechecks the saved Customer. The two rows can diverge.

**Android boundary:** HomeViewModel gets own accounts, picks the local preferred account or first; BalanceCard displays it with a dollar symbol even though backend Account currency is LKR; error state is not displayed by Home route. Three Recent Transactions are hardcoded examples; local swipe/Undo does not delete a bank transaction. Home Quick Actions callbacks and Notifications onClick are empty, while sidebar navigation reaches actual separate screens. My Accounts is real-data-backed with skeleton, refresh, error, empty, default-account DataStore and Customer-missing -> KYC/registration CTA. Android Profile GET/PUT /users/me reads/edits User fields only; generic email PUT initiates verification but UI optimistically shows new email before confirmation.

**Other profile/settings/summary boundaries:** Core has generic PUT /users/me consumed on Android but not web. Staff-only Customer list/update/delete belongs to Part 07, not the self-service profile. Core GET /transactions/my currently returns a literal string, not a dashboard activity list; staff AnalyticsController spending/fraud GETs return fixed text and are not a customer financial dashboard feed. No implemented customer preferences, notification-center backend, profile-photo, communication preferences, theme/language preferences or account-deletion request flow was found in inspected scope, so absent possibilities are noted without artificial Level 0 records. Android Settings includes existing App Lock (AUTH-012), logout (AUTH-005), and version info; device-local default account is DASH-015.

**Verification:** [web/playwright.config.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/playwright.config.ts) launches [web/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs); relevant [web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/smoke.spec.ts), [web/e2e/customer-onboarding.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/customer-onboarding.spec.ts), and [web/tests/customer-client.test.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/tests/customer-client.test.ts) exercise mock-core/contract/UI paths, not actual integrated Spring/MySQL. Android [app/app/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/ApiContractTest.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/ApiContractTest.kt) tests serialization, not device dashboard/profile. Spring integration tests were inspected but not run. No GitHub-accessible F1–F8 evidence specifically proves this Part 03 real-stack workflow. All ratings Source-confirmed, no Level 5.

#### DASH-001 — Web protected customer dashboard and entry

- **Review part:** 03.
- **User roles:** Authenticated CUSTOMER.
- **User objective:** Reach a role-scoped home after login.
- **Workflow boundary:** Web; defined goal only. No independent rating of AUTH/KYC or future financial modules.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Login/authenticated session -> roleHome(CUSTOMER) /customer -> customer layout ProtectedLayout -> AuthGuard verifies authenticated role; booting and boot-error states displayed, anonymous redirects /login?returnTo, other roles /unauthorized, reload revalidates. /customer/home is a redirect alias.
- **Frontend / Android:** [web/app/(customer)/customer/page.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/(customer)/customer/page.tsx); [web/app/(customer)/customer/layout.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/(customer)/customer/layout.tsx); [web/components/auth/AuthGuard.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/auth/AuthGuard.tsx)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/components/auth/AuthProvider.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/auth/AuthProvider.tsx); [web/lib/auth/navigation.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/auth/navigation.ts) Web protected API routes use HttpOnly-cookie BFF and CSRF on unsafe methods; Android direct requests use its bearer session. If no endpoint is listed, this is navigation/presentation only.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/interfaces/AuthController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/auth/interfaces/AuthController.java)
- **Database / ledger:** User.username and role, JWT/server session (AUTH-003/004/008); dashboard-specific state is client memory.
- **External dependencies:** No bank-service/FastAPI dependency in this dashboard/profile feature; KYC processing was covered in Part 02.
- **Authorization:** Web CUSTOMER layout or authenticated current User; Spring method/ownership checks apply independently.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Successful CUSTOMER sees protected shell; errors show retry; no direct banking mutations.
- **Existing automated tests:** [web/e2e/smoke.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/smoke.spec.ts) Tests are source evidence and have not been executed during this Part 03 review; Playwright uses mock bank-core, and Android serialization tests do not establish runtime UI behavior.
- **Available live verification:** None independently attributable to this feature in the GitHub source trees.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented**, for the stated channel/feature scope.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** Login/authenticated session -> roleHome(CUSTOMER) /customer -> customer layout ProtectedLayout -> AuthGuard verifies authenticated role; booting and boot-error states displayed, anonymous redirects /login?returnTo, other roles /unauthorized, reload revalidates.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** The guard is a navigation UX gate, not proof that linked banking workflows are complete.

#### DASH-006 — Banking Customer details maintenance accessible from onboarding

#### DASH-016 — Android My Accounts onboarding guidance

- **Review part:** 03.
- **User roles:** Logged-in Android user without Customer row.
- **User objective:** Navigate to identity verification or Customer registration.
- **Workflow boundary:** Android My Accounts (not Home); defined goal only. No independent rating of AUTH/KYC or future financial modules.
- **Complete workflow steps:** MyAccountsViewModel GET /accounts/my; AccountRepositoryImpl recognizes 404 message 'Customer not found', throws CustomerMissingException; ViewModel then checks KycRepository.myCase GET /kyc/me. KYC lookup failure treated as unknown and also shows Start Verification. Home itself does not load KYC/Customer state.
- **Frontend / Android:** [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/MyAccountsViewModel.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/MyAccountsViewModel.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/MyAccountsScreen.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/MyAccountsScreen.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/MyAccountsRoute.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/MyAccountsRoute.kt)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/repository/AccountRepositoryImpl.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/repository/AccountRepositoryImpl.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/integration/repository/KycRepositoryImpl.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/kyc/integration/repository/KycRepositoryImpl.kt) Web protected API routes use HttpOnly-cookie BFF and CSRF on unsafe methods; Android direct requests use its bearer session. If no endpoint is listed, this is navigation/presentation only.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycCaseController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/kyc/interfaces/KycCaseController.java)
- **Database / ledger:** Customer missing, KycCase latest status persists in Spring; Android UI state local.
- **External dependencies:** No bank-service/FastAPI dependency in this dashboard/profile feature; KYC processing was covered in Part 02.
- **Authorization:** Android session bearer; core business data uses authenticated-user/ownership or current User.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Register if KYC approved, otherwise verify; retryable account error on other failures.
- **Existing automated tests:** [web/e2e/customer-onboarding.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/customer-onboarding.spec.ts) Tests are source evidence and have not been executed during this Part 03 review; Playwright uses mock bank-core, and Android serialization tests do not establish runtime UI behavior.
- **Available live verification:** None independently attributable to this feature in the GitHub source trees.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 3 — Partially functional**, for the stated channel/feature scope.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** MyAccountsViewModel GET /accounts/my; AccountRepositoryImpl recognizes 404 message 'Customer not found', throws CustomerMissingException; ViewModel then checks KycRepository.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** No onboarding indicator on Home, no specific pending/rejected/RFI state in prompt, KYC error may be shown as needs verification.
- **Source references:** Commit-pinned source/test links are included in the feature record fields above.
- **Future implementation opportunities:** Surface KYC status accurately with unavailable/rejected distinctions on Android home or My Accounts.



### Part 04 — Customer account lifecycle

**Status:** COMPLETE — source-inspected feature completeness, not a real-stack acceptance claim.

**Reconfirmed GitHub main SHAs on 2026-11-23:** bank-web `012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27`; bank-core `9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3`; bank-service `cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3`; bank-app `9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38`. All four still match Part 03 and were pinned for this source review. bank-service is a KYC/ML service and no account-lifecycle Spring call to FastAPI exists: no unnecessary FastAPI feature attribution.

**Review boundary and nonduplication:** Account product rules, eligibility at account creation, customer and Teller opening, durable account-opening idempotency, ledger-funded creation, Account persistence and identifiers, owner/staff retrieval, staff metadata updates, status changes, zero-balance closure and Android opening. Existing Auth AUTH-003/004/008/010/011, KYC-011/012 and Dashboard DASH-002/014/015 are prerequisites/presentation, not re-rated. Deposits, journal accounting, transaction history, reversal and reconciliation detail are Part 05; Internal Pay Part 06; staff Customer management Part 07; complete Teller workspace Part 08; full Android Part 14.

**Actual product types and rules (hardcoded, not feature-complete real-world banking products):**

| Type | Core minimum | Opening deposit rule | Not established by named type | Exposed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| SAVINGS | 1000.00 LKR | Positive; up to 2 fractional digits | No interest/accrual/fees defined by Account opening | Web, staff and Android |
| CHECKING | 0.00 LKR | Zero allowed; positive amount posted via ledger | No overdraft/check-writing policy demonstrated | Web, staff and Android |
| FIXED_DEPOSIT | 5000.00 LKR | Positive; up to 2 fractional digits | No term/maturity/renewal/interest product operations identified | Web, staff and Android |

Core `AccountType` has three values and `AccountServiceImpl.MIN_DEPOSIT` holds their minimums. `AccountRequestDTO` requires accountType, initialDeposit and branchId; `@DecimalMin(0.00)` and service allow Checking zero even though its annotation message says greater than zero. Positive customer input uses `MonetaryAmount.fromCustomerInput` (no exponent, two decimals maximum, storage scale 4). Multiple accounts of same type are permitted with distinct keys; no max-account count or customer-status ACTIVE check was found. There is no interest, maturity, penalty or periodic-fee lifecycle implemented by these account-domain fields.

**Branches:** Branch is a persisted entity with numeric branchId, name, address, contact and optional manager. [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/support/bootstrap/BranchSeeder.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/support/bootstrap/BranchSeeder.java) seeds Colombo/Kandy/Galle if missing. Spring GET /api/v1/branches (CUSTOMER/TELLER/MANAGER/ADMIN) responds with all records; it currently ignores page/limit parameters. GET /branches/{id} resolves existing row. Web and Android load real branch choices and send integer branchId. Core requires existing branch for opening and staff edit, but has no branch.active field or branch-status gate. Account.branchId FK exists; AccountResponseDTO does not return it. BranchController's create/update/delete handlers are string stubs and do not represent operational branch administration; that feature belongs a later review.

**Eligibility:** Browser eligibility checks /customers/me then /kyc/me and routes to onboarding if required. Core repeats independently: POST /api/v1/accounts has CUSTOMER method authorization; authenticated User must map to a persisted banking Customer; the **latest** KycCase for that User must be APPROVED (KYC-012). Teller route POST /accounts/{userUuid} is TELLER only, resolves a linked Customer by targeted User UUID and deliberately bypasses that latest-KYC self-service requirement. Neither opening branch has a separate Customer.status ACTIVE gate; staff Customer existence and branch/type/deposit rules remain mandatory. Accounts can be created multiple times for the same user and same type by distinct operations.

**Actual web opening and retry:** /customer/accounts -> /customer/accounts/new renders CustomerAccountForm after eligibility check. It asynchronously fetches branches; chooses type, branch, decimal-text LKR initialDeposit; validates amounts and minimum, disables submission when pending; submits via accountsApi.createCustomerAccount POST /api/v1/accounts with Idempotency-Key and BFF CSRF; Spring responds 201 AccountResponseDTO; page displays account number/type/status/opening balance and GET /accounts/{uuid} fresh state, links account history and permits another opening. Errors distinguish auth, KYC/Customer, branch missing, minimum/precision, vault configuration and an uncertain transport outcome. An uncertain request triggers GET /accounts/my before allowing manual same-key retry; an empty list does not establish failed opening. Retry key and original payload stay only in page memory and are lost on reload; no automatic second opening with a new key.

**Idempotency guarantee:** Web randomUUID key per logical payload; same key retained for same operation; BFF forwards exact header. Core validates supported ASCII key length <=128 and hashes canonical `CUSTOMER_ACCOUNT_OPENING|type|branchId|deposit.stripTrailingZeros` with SHA-256. Unique MySQL (user_id,client_key) durable claim inserted `PROCESSING`, locked lookup on replay, `COMPLETED` stores account UUID + HTTP 201 + serialized AccountResponseDTO within @Transactional account creation/posting; same request replay 201, changed payload 409, still pending in-progress. Transactional failure rolls back claim and financial writes. Key is **optional** on direct Core, and neither Android nor Teller supplies one. V5 creates claim table with foreign keys and checks; V6 tightens completed-status nonnull check. No TTL/pruning policy or independent GET status/replay endpoint in inspected source. Unlike core transfer idempotency, this claim is specific to ACCOUNT OPENING and covers zero-funded and posted-funded opening.

**Account creation and funding:** AccountServiceImpl creates an ACTIVE account with initial structural 0.0000, currency LKR, linked Customer and Branch, 10-digit SecureRandom accountNumber and Hibernate-generated account_id UUID. Creates one-to-one liability LedgerAccount. For positive opening deposit it validates system `SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR` is ACTIVE ASSET and not customer-linked, then PostingEngine posts DEPOSIT (debit vault ASSET, credit customer LIABILITY), updates Account.balance via posting and inserts a journal entry, two postings and legacy transaction projection. For Checking zero, no posting/journal/legacy transaction row, but Account and liability LedgerAccount exist. One @Transactional opening wraps creation and posting, with source tests for rollback on missing vault, ledger save, posting and projection failure. This is internal accounting; no external payment confirmation or physical cash receipt is established. Exact ledger integrity will be Part 05.

**Account identifiers and storage:**

| Identifier | Source/storage | Use | Not interchangeable with |
|---|---|---|---|
| User UUID | users.user_id | Login identity/current principal used for owner resolution and staff POST /accounts/{id} target | Not bank account number |
| Customer UUID | customers.customer_id | Distinct banking Customer record linked uniquely to User; stored as Account.customer_id FK | Not accepted as Teller target in POST /accounts/{id} |
| Account UUID | accounts.account_id | Binary(16) primary key, Hibernate UUID generator; /accounts/{id}, detail/history/close | Not human account number |
| Account number | accounts.account_number | SecureRandom 10-digit display identifier, DB UNIQUE; prechecked 10 attempts | No /accounts/by-number endpoint found |
| Branch ID | branches.branch_id | Integer FK from Account.branch_id, selected from live list | Not returned by AccountResponseDTO |
| Customer liability LedgerAccount UUID | ledger_accounts.ledger_account_id | Separate ledger posting account linked uniquely to Account UUID | Not exposed in account response |

`AccountResponseDTO` returns accountId, accountNumber, accountType, accountStatus, balance, createdAt, updatedAt. It does **not** return currency, branch, Customer UUID/User UUID, ledger ID or available-versus-posted balance. Account.currency defaults to LKR, allowed format constrained by V2, service sets LKR on opening. MySQL accounts table includes UNIQUE accountNumber, FK to Customer and Branch, Account.balance DECIMAL(19,4) and Account status; ledger_accounts has unique customer_account_id. Pre-check generation loops up to 10 times, but a database unique collision from concurrent account-number generation would reject rather than automatically regenerate that saved entity.

**HTTP domain operation and role matrix:**

| Operation | API or internal service | Authorized role | Actual restriction or result |
|---|---|---|---|
| Customer own-account opening | POST /api/v1/accounts | CUSTOMER | Latest approved KYC, current User-linked Customer, valid branch/type/deposit; optional Idempotency-Key |
| Teller-assisted opening | POST /api/v1/accounts/{userUuid} | TELLER | Target linked User; Customer exists; same branch/minimum rules, no latest KYC requirement and no Idempotency-Key |
| Owner listing | GET /api/v1/accounts/my | CUSTOMER | Current User -> Customer -> all linked accounts including CLOSED, no filter/page |
| Single account / history | GET /api/v1/accounts/{uuid}; /{uuid}/transactions | Owner OR ADMIN/MANAGER/TELLER | Server ownership expression controls account-by-ID and history |
| Staff directory | GET /api/v1/accounts | ADMIN/MANAGER/TELLER | All accounts, no pagination or search |
| Metadata editing | PUT /api/v1/accounts/{uuid} | ADMIN/MANAGER/TELLER | Type and branch updates; no balance mutation |
| Closure | DELETE /api/v1/accounts/{uuid} | ADMIN/MANAGER/TELLER | ACTIVE and zero stored+ledger-derived balance; 204; retained record/history |
| Freeze/reopen status | AccountService.changeAccountStatus internal method | No HTTP role because endpoint absent | Backend-only service; not actual customer/staff UI workflow |

GET /accounts/my has no pagination/status filter: historical CLOSED rows remain visible. Account by UUID checks ownership in Spring before GET; account number is display-only, no by-number lookup route. Staff account directory /accounts is an unpaginated findAll. Web HistoryView (account-scoped) GETs both account detail and its transactions; closed account owners can still see history. The browser can export current history to CSV, but Part 05 will review full transaction-query correctness.

**Status and reachable transition matrix:**

| State | Entered through | Actual operation | Visible/reachable result | Potential next state / limit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ACTIVE | All newly created accounts | Declared enum and openAccount initializes | Customer/Staff GET; PostingEngine allows transaction after authorization | Staff DELETE can move to CLOSED if dual-zero; internal status method can set FROZEN |
| FROZEN | Internal AccountService.changeAccountStatus or test fixture; no exposed status API | Service changes Account.status under row lock; PostingEngine rejects non-ACTIVE | Visible as read-only status in web/Android | Service can set ACTIVE or CLOSED invokes close checks, but no connected UI/admin API |
| CLOSED | Staff DELETE /accounts/{uuid}, internal changeAccountStatus(CLOSED) | ACTIVE zero balances (Account & derived ledger), current row/ledger integrity; repeated CLOSED close no-op | Still in /my list, staff directory, GET by UUID, history; PostingEngine rejects | No public reopen; internal changeAccountStatus(ACTIVE) not guarded against closed-to-active |

For closure, staff UI requires displayed ACTIVE + zero, explicit review/confirmation, fresh account read and DELETE. Spring checks under row PESSIMISTIC_WRITE lock the refreshed stored balance, status, ledger liability mapping/class/currency and independently derived ledger liability total zero. Financial PostingEngine uses the same underlying Account row lock and rejects non-ACTIVE accounts; race tests model both directions. Core DELETE /accounts/{uuid} produces 204, CLOSED row persists; repeated close at zero is no-op with unchanged updatedAt. LedgerAccount.status stays ACTIVE on closure; historical journal/postings/legacy transaction rows remain. Frozen account is not closeable. Customer closure is absent. `changeAccountStatus(ACTIVE/FROZEN)` exists only as internal service method, not mapped HTTP API; there is no source-confirmed client freeze/unfreeze/reopen path.

**Staff metadata management:** StaffAccountDetail can review and PUT AccountType and Branch. `AccountRequestDTO` is reused and requires initialDeposit though updateAccount ignores it; web fills current balance string after fresh GET and performs mutation without moving money. AccountServiceImpl uses pessimistic row lock, sets type and chosen Branch and updatedAt. There is no account-status edit in this PUT, customer-editable metadata, owner reassignment, nickname, server version compare-and-swap or change-specific account-type policy. Updates can apply to CLOSED account type/branch, but no reopen method is exposed. Response cannot verify current Branch because Branch isn't in DTO. Detailed staff UI in Part 08.

**Web versus Android:** Web uses lossless decimal string, conditional eligibility preflight, randomUUID Idempotency-Key, exact payload/key replay and state-aware outcome/recovery. Android OpenAccountScreen/ViewModel/Retrofit has three correct types and branch dropdown plus Core-connected POST, but amount is converted to Kotlin Double and serialized as Double, Idempotency-Key is absent, no ambiguous-response reconciliation/key reuse and no prior latest-KYC preflight. OpenAccountScreen has optional `kycRequired` banner default false; AccountsRoute does not bind it. Android can show Customer-missing KYC snackbar action, but its failure handling does not distinguish all KYC statuses. Android My Accounts list and local default-account choice were reviewed in DASH-014/015, not scored again. Android does not expose account type/branch edit, freeze, close or detailed account read; history route remains navigable.

**Evidence/test types:** [web/playwright.config.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/playwright.config.ts) launches [web/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs) for all browser Playwright source tests (including [web/e2e/customer-account-opening.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/customer-account-opening.spec.ts), [web/e2e/staff-account-opening.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/staff-account-opening.spec.ts) and [web/e2e/staff-account-management.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/staff-account-management.spec.ts)), so those are **mock-bank-core browser scenarios**, not actual Spring/MySQL. Vitest account-opening, API, staff-detail and idempotency tests are mocked unit/component contracts. [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java) and [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountClosureIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountClosureIntegrationTest.java) are SpringBootTest/MockMvc database-backed integration test *source*, covering posting/rollback/concurrent key use, zero-balance, locking and retained history, but they were not executed here, and no attributable passing output was available. BranchLookupMvcTest uses mock BranchRepository. Android ApiContractTest covers serialization rather than device account opening. No Part 04 F1–F8 attributable real-stack report was independently available. All Part 04 confidence remains **Source-confirmed**, no L5.

#### ACC-001 — Account types and minimum-deposit product rules

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER and TELLER account openers.
- **User objective:** Choose actual supported account type under its opening amount rule.
- **Workflow boundary:** Choose actual supported account type under its opening amount rule; CUSTOMER and TELLER account openers. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Spring enum declares SAVINGS, CHECKING, FIXED_DEPOSIT and AccountServiceImpl MIN_DEPOSIT map sets 1000.00, 0.00, 5000.00 LKR respectively. Both web customer/Teller forms expose exactly those three and validate minimums; Android exposes enum-aligned three types and minimum hints. Core checks every opening, including Teller. These are enum+hardcoded service rules, not independently configured banking products.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/lib/finance/account-opening.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/finance/account-opening.ts); [web/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx); [web/components/banking/StaffAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/StaffAccountForm.tsx) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountScreen.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountScreen.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountViewModel.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountViewModel.kt)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/accounts.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/accounts.ts); [web/app/api/v1/[...path]/route.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/api/v1/%5B...path%5D/route.ts) Protected web calls use cookie-auth BFF/CSRF and bearer forwarding; Android uses direct Retrofit bearer. The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/enums/AccountType.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/modules/common/enums/AccountType.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java)
- **Database / ledger:** Account.accountType varchar/enum stored in accounts; no account-product configuration entity or interest/fee/maturity columns for this type in Account model.
- **External dependencies:** MySQL persistence and internal ledger/posting where noted; no FastAPI/bank-service, third-party payment processor, or external cash-settlement interaction in the account-opening code.
- **Authorization:** CUSTOMER self-service or TELLER assisted roles, enforced at distinct AccountController POST routes.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Too-small opening deposit gets ERR_MIN_DEPOSIT before persistence; valid Checking zero accepted; account type selected in request then stored/returned.
- **Existing automated tests:** [web/tests/account-opening.test.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/tests/account-opening.test.ts); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java) Source inspected but tests not run in Part 04; web Playwright uses mock bank-core; Spring @SpringBootTest/MockMvc tests are source-only evidence, not observed passing runs.
- **Available live verification:** None attributable to actual integrated-stack Part 04 workflow in inspected GitHub repositories.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented** for stated feature and role scope, not a broader product certification.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** Spring enum declares SAVINGS, CHECKING, FIXED_DEPOSIT and AccountServiceImpl MIN_DEPOSIT map sets 1000.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** Type labels do not prove interest accrual, fixed-deposit tenure/maturity, penalties, fees or individualized product terms; no subtype-specific operations or same-type account count limit.
- **Source references:** Commit-pinned source links in fields above; relevant Flyway migrations are [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql) and [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql).
- **Future implementation opportunities:** Decide whether product definitions require separate configurable terms, interest, maturity and fee rules.


#### ACC-002 — Persisted branch directory and account-opening branch selection

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER, TELLER and authorized staff readers.
- **User objective:** Select a real branch when opening or editing accounts.
- **Workflow boundary:** Select a real branch when opening or editing accounts; CUSTOMER, TELLER and authorized staff readers. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** BranchSeeder creates Colombo, Kandy and Galle examples if absent. BranchController GET /api/v1/branches and /{id} return Branch entities from repository. Web accountsApi.branches GET /branches?page=1&limit=100, selects branch from bank-core list with address/phone, sends integer branchId. Android loads GET /branches to dropdown and sends integer branch ID. Core opening and metadata update resolve BranchRepository.findById; Account.branch is persisted foreign key.

#### ACC-003 — Core-enforced self-service account-opening eligibility

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** Authenticated CUSTOMER.
- **User objective:** Prohibit own account creation without banking Customer and latest approved KYC.
- **Workflow boundary:** Prohibit own account creation without banking Customer and latest approved KYC; Authenticated CUSTOMER. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Web useAccountOpeningEligibility GET /customers/me then latest /kyc/me and offers onboarding vs opening, but backend is authoritative. AccountController POST /accounts requires CUSTOMER; AccountServiceImpl.openCustomerAccount resolves current authenticated User and checks role, openAccount finds Customer by User.userId and only for non-Teller checks latest KycCase.APPROVED. Missing Customer 404; missing/nonapproved latest KYC ERR_KYC_NOT_APPROVED; null customer/branch does not produce account. Core does not separately test Customer.status ACTIVE or maximum accounts.

#### ACC-004 — Web customer self-service account opening and confirmation

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** Authenticated eligible CUSTOMER.
- **User objective:** Open an owned account from account selection to confirmed receipt.
- **Workflow boundary:** Open an owned account from account selection to confirmed receipt; Authenticated eligible CUSTOMER. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** /customer/accounts -> /customer/accounts/new, useAccountOpeningEligibility checks Customer and latest KYC -> branches GET -> choose SAVINGS/CHECKING/FIXED_DEPOSIT, branch and opening deposit decimal text -> client validates amount/type/branch -> POST /api/v1/accounts with body {accountType,initialDeposit,branchId} + Idempotency-Key, CSRF via Next BFF -> Spring AccountService -> Customer Account + ledger -> HTTP 201 response with account UUID, number, status, type, balance, timestamps -> web validates confirmation, GET /accounts/{uuid} for fresh status/balance -> displays receipt and account history link, allows new logical opening.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/app/(customer)/customer/accounts/new/page.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/(customer)/customer/accounts/new/page.tsx); [web/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx); [web/components/banking/CustomerAccounts.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccounts.tsx) Android: Not applicable in this feature's scoped channel.
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/accounts.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/accounts.ts); [web/lib/api/http.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/http.ts); [web/lib/finance/account-opening.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/finance/account-opening.ts); [web/app/api/v1/[...path]/route.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/api/v1/%5B...path%5D/route.ts) Protected web calls use cookie-auth BFF/CSRF and bearer forwarding; Android uses direct Retrofit bearer. The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.

#### ACC-005 — Durable idempotent self-service opening and uncertain-response recovery

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER using web, direct Core client with optional Idempotency-Key.
- **User objective:** Prevent repeated account and initial funding writes for the same logical request.
- **Workflow boundary:** Prevent repeated account and initial funding writes for the same logical request; CUSTOMER using web, direct Core client with optional Idempotency-Key. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Web lib/finance/idempotency.ts generates crypto.randomUUID for logical payload and keeps key+payload in component memory; locks duplicate clicks. BFF forwards Idempotency-Key. Spring AccountServiceImpl validates IdempotencyKey when provided and SHA-256 fingerprints accountType|branchId|normalized initialDeposit (stripTrailingZeros). IdempotencyService INSERT IGNORE unique (user_id,client_key) PROCESSING claim and locked lookup serialize creation/funding + final HTTP 201 AccountResponseDTO in same @Transactional boundary. Same fingerprint replays same response, changed request returns 409 ERR_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT, still PROCESSING signals in-progress. On network uncertainty web GET /accounts/my, warns empty result not proof of failure, only enables deliberate same-key retry after read, and supports conflict review. Failed transactional opening rolls back claim and account/ledger writes. No key -> backward-compatible unprotected creation. No TTL/prune or separate GET idempotency status endpoint.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/api/AccountsApi.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/api/AccountsApi.kt)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/finance/idempotency.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/finance/idempotency.ts); [web/lib/api/accounts.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/accounts.ts); [web/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts) Protected web calls use cookie-auth BFF/CSRF and bearer forwarding; Android uses direct Retrofit bearer. The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.
