#### ACC-006 — Account identifiers, ownership and atomic account persistence

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER/TELLER opening, Core persistence.
- **User objective:** Create a distinct bank account linked to the correct Customer and Branch.
- **Workflow boundary:** Create a distinct bank account linked to the correct Customer and Branch; CUSTOMER/TELLER opening, Core persistence. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Core generates 10-digit human-readable account number using SecureRandom, checking AccountRepository.existsByAccountNumber up to 10 attempts; DB UNIQUE account_number enforces uniqueness. Hibernate @UuidGenerator assigns account_id binary UUID, separate from Customer.customer_id UUID and User.user_id UUID and integer Branch.branch_id. Service creates ACTIVE Account with LKR, structural 0.0000 balance, UTC timestamps and Customer+Branch FKs, savesAndFlushes then creates one-to-one customer liability LedgerAccount; optional funding posts. All in @Transactional service; no account write if failing startup validation or posting; rollback tests exist.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx); [web/components/banking/StaffAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/StaffAccountForm.tsx) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/dto/AccountNet.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/dto/AccountNet.kt)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/accounts.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/accounts.ts) Protected web calls use cookie-auth BFF/CSRF and bearer forwarding; Android uses direct Retrofit bearer. The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/Account.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/Account.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/repository/AccountRepository.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/repository/AccountRepository.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/customer/domain/Customer.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/customer/domain/Customer.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/branch/domain/Branch.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/branch/domain/Branch.java)
- **Database / ledger:** accounts (account_id UUID, account_number UNIQUE, account_type, account_status, balance DECIMAL(19,4), currency LKR, customer_id FK, branch_id FK, timestamps); ledger_accounts UNIQUE customer_account_id, V1/V2 baseline/migration.
- **External dependencies:** MySQL persistence and internal ledger/posting where noted; no FastAPI/bank-service, third-party payment processor, or external cash-settlement interaction in the account-opening code.
- **Authorization:** Teller targets linked User UUID to resolve Customer; customer self-service resolves own User/Customer, never trusts client owner UUID.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** AccountResponseDTO exposes accountId, accountNumber,type,status,balance,createdAt,updatedAt, but not Customer/Branch UUID, currency or ledger-account ID.
- **Existing automated tests:** [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/AccountCurrencyTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/AccountCurrencyTest.java) Source inspected but tests not run in Part 04; web Playwright uses mock bank-core; Spring @SpringBootTest/MockMvc tests are source-only evidence, not observed passing runs.

- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented** for stated feature and role scope, not a broader product certification.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** Core generates 10-digit human-readable account number using SecureRandom, checking AccountRepository.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** 10 attempts precheck does not itself guarantee automatic retry of a concurrent DB unique collision; database UNIQUE rejects collision, no caught regenerating save loop; account response omits owner and branch references. Multiple same-type accounts allowed (distinct logical operations).
- **Source references:** Commit-pinned source links in fields above; relevant Flyway migrations are [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql) and [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql).

#### ACC-007 — Opening funding and liability-ledger integration

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER or TELLER opening with supplied initialDeposit.
- **User objective:** Initialize account money state consistently with ledger.
- **Workflow boundary:** Initialize account money state consistently with ledger; CUSTOMER or TELLER opening with supplied initialDeposit. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** AccountRequestDTO requires non-null BigDecimal >=0. Core MIN_DEPOSIT by type; positive amount is MonetaryAmount.fromCustomerInput decimal plain text (max 2 fractional digits, no silent rounding), converted to storage scale four. Funded opening requires ACTIVE ASSET SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR ledger account (V3 cutover). Service creates Account at zero, customer LIABILITY ledger account, then PostingEngine.post DEPOSIT with debit vault and credit customer; PostingEngine writes journal_entry/journal_postings, updates stored Account.balance and legacy transaction projection. Zero Checking creates Account+LedgerAccount only and no journal/transaction row. All within @Transactional opening and claim if key. Failing vault/posting/projection prevents persistence.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx); [web/lib/finance/account-opening.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/finance/account-opening.ts) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountViewModel.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountViewModel.kt)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/accounts.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/accounts.ts); [web/lib/api/http.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/http.ts) Protected web calls use cookie-auth BFF/CSRF and bearer forwarding; Android uses direct Retrofit bearer. The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/domain/MonetaryAmount.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/domain/MonetaryAmount.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/domain/repository/JournalPostingRepository.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/domain/repository/JournalPostingRepository.java)
- **Database / ledger:** accounts.balance DECIMAL(19,4); ledger_accounts liability mapping; journal_entries DEPOSIT+2 journal_postings; legacy transactions projection for funded opening; V2/V3/V4 migrations.
- **External dependencies:** MySQL persistence and internal ledger/posting where noted; no FastAPI/bank-service, third-party payment processor, or external cash-settlement interaction in the account-opening code.
- **Authorization:** Account-opening role and ownership checks before posting; PostingEngine locks account rows and checks ACTIVE/Currency to update balances.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Successful funded opening returns posted balance, zero Checking returns 0.0000; insufficient minimum ERR_MIN_DEPOSIT, invalid scale ERR_DEPOSIT_INVALID, absent/invalid vault ERR_VAULT_ACCOUNT_NOT_FOUND/INVALID. No external cash settlement collection API is called by opening.
- **Existing automated tests:** [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/AccountOpeningIntegrationTest.java); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/PostingEngineIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/PostingEngineIntegrationTest.java) Source inspected but tests not run in Part 04; web Playwright uses mock bank-core; Spring @SpringBootTest/MockMvc tests are source-only evidence, not observed passing runs.
- **Available live verification:** None attributable to actual integrated-stack Part 04 workflow in inspected GitHub repositories.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented** for stated feature and role scope, not a broader product certification.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** AccountRequestDTO requires non-null BigDecimal >=0.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** The posting debits a pre-existing system vault account; real source of cash/deposit collection is not independently verified, no external funding authorization. Deeper ledger correctness is deferred to Part 05. Android uses Double amount (precision contract differs).
- **Source references:** Commit-pinned source links in fields above; relevant Flyway migrations are [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql) and [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql).
- **Future implementation opportunities:** Define physical/external funding provenance and preserve decimal exactness in mobile clients.


#### ACC-008 — Teller-assisted opening with linked Customer user

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** TELLER staff operator; beneficiary existing Customer.
- **User objective:** Open account for selected banking Customer without customer self-service eligibility sequence.
- **Workflow boundary:** Open account for selected banking Customer without customer self-service eligibility sequence; TELLER staff operator; beneficiary existing Customer. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** StaffAccountForm /staff/accounts/new loads staff customer directory + branch list, filters Customer DTOs with linked userId and chooses User UUID (not Customer.customerId), type, branch, decimal deposit; calls POST /api/v1/accounts/{userId} (TELLER only). AccountServiceImpl.openAccount detects teller, finds Customer by target User UUID, skips customer's latest KYC approval check but enforces branch/type/minimum/funding. Same core account and ledger persistence; returns 201 AccountResponseDTO. UI refreshes /accounts/{accountId}, links history; on ambiguous network response disables retry and instructs staff reconciliation because Teller endpoint lacks idempotency/key and customer-account matching endpoint.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/app/(staff)/staff/accounts/new/page.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/(staff)/staff/accounts/new/page.tsx); [web/components/banking/StaffAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/StaffAccountForm.tsx) Android: Not applicable in this feature's scoped channel.
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/accounts.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/accounts.ts); [web/lib/api/staff.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/staff.ts) Protected web calls use cookie-auth BFF/CSRF and bearer forwarding; Android uses direct Retrofit bearer. The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/interfaces/AccountController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/interfaces/AccountController.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java)
- **Database / ledger:** Same Account and ledger accounts, posted opening deposit if positive; recipient account FK points to Customer resolved from linked User UUID.
- **External dependencies:** MySQL persistence and internal ledger/posting where noted; no FastAPI/bank-service, third-party payment processor, or external cash-settlement interaction in the account-opening code.
- **Authorization:** Spring @PreAuthorize TELLER on POST /accounts/{id}; self-service CUSTOMER route distinct. Staff Customer directory roles ADMIN/TELLER/MANAGER to choose target.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Success receipt with chosen customer/branch local context and core account fields; missing/unlinked User/Customer or branch rejects; uncertain response stops unsafe automatic retry.

#### ACC-009 — Owner-scoped account list and account UUID detail retrieval

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER self-read, authorized staff lookup.

- **Workflow boundary:** Retrieve own accounts and individual account current state by UUID; CUSTOMER self-read, authorized staff lookup. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.

GET /api/v1/accounts/{accountUuid} requires ADMIN/TELLER/MANAGER or @securityService.isAccountOwner(authentication,#id). Web CustomerAccounts shows list and links to /customer/accounts/{uuid}/transactions; HistoryView calls GET /accounts/{uuid} AND /accounts/{uuid}/transactions to show current balance and accountId. Android My Accounts uses /accounts/my and navigates to history. Closed accounts remain in list and accessible by UUID; details DTO has timestamps but omits currency, owner and branch.

#### ACC-012 — Staff-only zero-balance account closure with posting-compatible lock

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** ADMIN, MANAGER, TELLER; CUSTOMER forbidden.
- **User objective:** Close ACTIVE bank account only when balances settled to zero.
- **Workflow boundary:** Close ACTIVE bank account only when balances settled to zero; ADMIN, MANAGER, TELLER; CUSTOMER forbidden. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** StaffAccountDetail allows Close when status ACTIVE and displayed stored balance string zero; requires confirmation and fresh GET snapshot, sends DELETE /api/v1/accounts/{uuid} via CSRF BFF. Spring AccountController staff-only -> AccountServiceImpl.closeAccount @Transactional -> AccountRepository.findByIdForUpdate + EntityManager.refresh with PESSIMISTIC_WRITE. Under lock first checks Account.balance = 0, status ACTIVE (or already CLOSED), validates mapped liability LedgerAccount currency/class/status, and journalPostingRepository.calculateDerivedLiabilityBalance=0. If valid set accountStatus CLOSED + updatedAt; no DELETE physical row, no journal to close. Pending financial posting uses same lock/status checks; duplicate close after CLOSED returns 204 without timestamp mutation. UI readback confirms closed or flags uncertainty without sending second DELETE.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/StaffAccountDetail.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/StaffAccountDetail.tsx) Android: Not applicable in this feature's scoped channel.

#### ACC-013 — Backend-only AccountStatus change and freeze/reactivation service

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** Internal service caller (no exposed API path confirmed).
- **User objective:** Change account lifecycle status outside closure.
- **Workflow boundary:** Change account lifecycle status outside closure; Internal service caller (no exposed API path confirmed). Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** AccountStatus enum defines ACTIVE, FROZEN, CLOSED. AccountServiceImpl.changeAccountStatus(UUID,status) locks row and if CLOSED invokes full zero-balance closure checks; for other statuses simply sets Account.accountStatus and updatedAt (including ACTIVE on previously CLOSED Account), without an additional transition guard. PostingEngine independently rejects account status not ACTIVE. No mapped AccountController PATCH/status endpoint, no UI freeze/unfreeze/reopen control in reviewed web/Android source; UpdateAccountStatusDTO is empty class. Staff PUT /accounts/{id} changes type/branch but not status.

#### ACC-015 — Android native account opening and mobile completeness gap

- **Review part:** 04.
- **User roles:** Authenticated Android CUSTOMER.
- **User objective:** Open one of three account types with selected branch on phone.
- **Workflow boundary:** Open one of three account types with selected branch on phone; Authenticated Android CUSTOMER. Account-domain scope only: inherited AUTH/KYC/DASH journeys and full later financial/staff/mobile coverage not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** AppNavHost Open Account -> AccountsRoute/OpenAccountViewModel loads GET /branches, user selects AccountType, branch, enters initialDeposit string, ViewModel validates by converting to Double and compares local minimums, then AccountRepositoryImpl sends Retrofit POST /api/v1/accounts with AccountOpenRequest {accountType,initialDeposit:Double,branchId:Int}. Spring AccountController + AccountServiceImpl enforce same User/Customer/latest approved KYC and Core rules. On HTTP success accountId+accountNumber event triggers snackbar and navigates per-account history; errors emitted as snackbar and missing Customer offers Start KYC action. Native OpenAccountScreen has kycRequired flag but AccountsRoute does not pass it (defaults false), so pre-submit banner is not wired. Retrofit has no Idempotency-Key and no saved logical request/durable uncertain-result reconciliation; repeated attempts may create another account after ambiguous response.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/CustomerAccountForm.tsx) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/app/navigation/AppNavHost.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/app/navigation/AppNavHost.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/AccountsRoute.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/AccountsRoute.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountScreen.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountScreen.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountViewModel.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/OpenAccountViewModel.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/api/AccountsApi.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/api/AccountsApi.kt); [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/dto/AccountNet.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/integration/remote/dto/AccountNet.kt)

The exact methods, paths, request fields, responses and recovery steps appear in the complete trace above.

- **Existing automated tests:** [app/app/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/ApiContractTest.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/ApiContractTest.kt); [web/e2e/customer-account-opening.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/customer-account-opening.spec.ts) Source inspected but tests not run in Part 04; web Playwright uses mock bank-core; Spring @SpringBootTest/MockMvc tests are source-only evidence, not observed passing runs.
- **Available live verification:** None attributable to actual integrated-stack Part 04 workflow in inspected GitHub repositories.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 3 — Partially functional** for stated feature and role scope, not a broader product certification.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** AppNavHost Open Account -> AccountsRoute/OpenAccountViewModel loads GET /branches, user selects AccountType, branch, enters initialDeposit string, ViewModel validates by converting to Double and compares local minimums, then AccountRepositoryImpl sends Retrofit POST /api/v1/accounts with AccountOpenRequest {accountType,initialDeposit:Double,branchId:Int}.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** Mobile Double input/JSON loses exact decimal-text representation, may reject >2 fractional digits only at server; no idempotency header, no uncertain-response readback/replay, preflight KYC banner not bound, no native closure or account metadata edit.
- **Source references:** Commit-pinned source links in fields above; relevant Flyway migrations are [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V1__baseline.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V5__customer_account_opening_idempotency.sql) and [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V6__tighten_customer_account_opening_idempotency_result_check.sql).

### Part 05 — Banking transactions and double-entry ledger

**Status:** COMPLETE — GitHub source-inspected feature-completeness review only, not an observed live integrated-stack financial acceptance.

**Reconfirmed main HEAD on 2026-11-24:** bank-web `012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27`; bank-core `9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3`; bank-service `cabdd323b4aa394910cb192ead6d233e9a12d9d3`; bank-app `9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38`. The inspected web, Core and Android source is commit-pinned to these unchanged main snapshots. No real financial dependency from bank-core to bank-service KYC/FastAPI was found; bank-service ref was confirmed but not used to invent ledger execution. All three relevant GitHub recursive trees were non-truncated.

**Boundaries and nonduplication:** Part 05 traces actually supported customer deposit/withdrawal, financial owner and state validation, ledger accounts and double-entry journals, numerical accuracy, atomicity and concurrency, durable idempotency, transaction history, backend-only reconciliation and reversal contract, staff read paths, Android payment preview versus real account history, and migration/cutover architecture. Account opening/idempotency/closure already ACC-004/005/007/012; web account cards DASH-002; native Home recent demo DASH-009. The *complete* Internal Pay workflow and transfer receipt recovery are Part 06; full Teller operations Part 08; Admin reversal UI Part 12; full Android app Part 14; platform operations Part 15.

**Transaction domain map:**

`Web customer /customer/transactions/deposit|withdraw → FinancialForm → transactionsApi + CSRF Next BFF /api/v1/[...path] → Spring TransactionController (CUSTOMER; authenticated User UUID) → FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl (owner/ACTIVE/LKR/ledger validation) → CoreIdempotencyCoordinator + bounded fresh-transaction retry → PostingEngineImpl (customer account PESSIMISTIC_WRITE, balanced legs, no overdraft) → journal_entries/journal_postings, Account.balance and mandatory legacy Transaction projection → HTTP 200 DepositReceipt/WithdrawalReceipt → browser journal ref, GET account balance and history.`

`Android PaymentsPicker → DepositScreen/WithdrawScreen → PaymentDraft → PaymentReviewScreen → PaymentReceiptScreen` is **local UI-only** and performs no financial POST. Its *separate* My Accounts transaction-history route uses actual Retrofit GET /accounts/{uuid}/transactions.

**Real posting/economic effects:**

| Operation | Debit ledger | Credit ledger | Account/history effect | Response and boundary |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Web deposit POST /api/v1/transactions/deposit | SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR ASSET | Customer LIABILITY | Stored Account balance +amount; 1 DEPOSIT history projection | DepositReceipt HTTP 200 with posted journal ID/ref |
| Web withdrawal POST /api/v1/transactions/withdraw | Customer LIABILITY | SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR ASSET | Stored Account balance -amount; insufficient funds rejects | WithdrawalReceipt HTTP 200 with posted journal ID/ref |
| Internal Pay shared ledger (Part 06 later) | Source Customer LIABILITY | Destination Customer LIABILITY | Source -amount; destination +amount; 2 history rows | TransferReceipt; full customer Pay later |
| Account opening funded (ACC-007 already reviewed) | SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR ASSET | New Customer LIABILITY | 0 -> deposit balance; 1 history projection | Opening AccountResponseDTO, details Part04 |
| Legacy cutover V3, opening-balance migration | SYSTEM_OPENING_BALANCE:LKR ASSET | Existing Customer LIABILITY | Balances preserved; no new legacy history rows | POSTED OPENING_BALANCE journal, system actor |
| Admin reversal POST /transactions/{journalEntryId}/reversal | Inverse of original credit posting | Inverse of original debit posting | Opposite signed customer effects (if no overdraft) | New REVERSAL journal and Replay receipt; Part12 full admin |

**Money origin and settlement semantics:** Deposits are internally backed by debit to pre-existing `SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR` ASSET account and credit to Customer LIABILITY. Withdrawals debit Customer LIABILITY and credit the same vault. Code proves balanced *internal* ledger booking, not physical cash acceptance, cash payout, bank/provider collection, provider settlement or transaction reversal through an external rail. Do not classify as settled external money movement.

**Financial authorization and eligibility:** TransactionController's POST /deposit, /withdraw and /transfer enforce CUSTOMER. AuthenticatedUserIdProvider supplies User UUID; FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl requires Account belongs to Customer found by User UUID, Account.status ACTIVE, Account.currency LKR, correct ACTIVE customer LIABILITY LedgerAccount and ACTIVE LKR ASSET vault. PostingEngine rechecks affected Account status/currency after row locking. Wrong owner returns ERR_ACCOUNT_ACCESS_DENIED, frozen/closed ERR_ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVE, insufficient funds ERR_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS (422). The account type does not impose separate transaction rules, and these operations do *not* recheck latest KYC or Customer.status; eligibility at opening is separate ACC-003. The customer-facing forms load /accounts/my, but server enforcement is independent of the UI account selector. Teller/Admin/Manager have staff account history GET, **not** POST deposit/withdraw permission on the same customer commands.

**Posting invariants and database structure:** `ledger_accounts` model customer LIABILITY or system ASSET/EQUITY identity with exclusive customerAccountId/systemCode, currency and separate status. `journal_entries` immutable JPA header has UUID `entry_id`, unique `entry_reference`, entry type, `POSTED` status, LKR, positive `total_amount`, initiating actor, channel, postedAt, optional reversalOf original. `journal_postings` immutable rows carry posting UUID, entry UUID FK, ledger UUID FK, direction DEBIT/CREDIT, positive amount, sequence number 0..n and currency. The application validates total debit == total credit and journal amount <= DECIMAL(19,4); V2 database CHECK/UNIQUE/FKs support structural integrity, **not cross-row aggregate balancing by themselves**. PostingEngine is single normal production Account.balance writer; projection rows created synchronously.

**Precision and currencies:** Web transaction inputs stay decimal strings all the way to JSON, Spring `StrictDecimalTextDeserializer` rejects numeric JSON tokens, `MonetaryAmount.fromCustomerInput` accepts only positive plain decimals with at most 2 fractional digits and no silent rounding; internal ledger uses 4 fractional digits, SQL DECIMAL(19,4), PostingEngine max 999999999999999.9999. CurrencyCode supports ISO-4217 values, but user financial workflows and system vault are hardcoded LKR, with no functional cross-currency/FX. Account balance and transaction view return materialized decimal values; web HTTP preserves exact numeric JSON decimals as strings. Android TransactionNet converts amount and balanceAfter to Double and visually adds '+' to positive magnitudes regardless of withdrawal direction.

**Atomicity, contention and retry:** PostingEngine gathers affected Customer Account UUIDs, canonical sorts, takes `AccountRepository.findByIdForUpdate` pessimistic locks and refreshes managed entities before status/currency/balance checks. Liability DEBIT decreases Account.balance; CREDIT increases. Negative result aborts as insufficient funds; Account.balance, immutable journal/posting legs, legacy transactions projection and Core idempotency completion persist in one physical transaction; failed projection and posting inserts roll back. Transfer locks two accounts in UUID order, closure uses matching row lock. BoundedFinancialTransactionRetryExecutor makes at most three separate REQUIRES_NEW attempts on MySQL 1213 or SQLSTATE 40001 with 25/50ms backoff; lock-wait timeout 1205 is deliberately not retried. Exhaustion is 503 ERR_TRANSACTION_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE. Source tests explicitly model concurrent withdrawals, opposite transfers, rollback and deadlock behavior, but no tests were executed.

**Durable financial idempotency vs account-opening idempotency:** Deposit and Withdrawal `Idempotency-Key` is effectively required (controller header annotated optional but calls requireKey, so absent/invalid → 400); web randomUUID per operation and forwards through CSRF BFF. Core fingerprint includes operation type, accountId(s), normalized LKR amount; MySQL `core_transaction_idempotency` UNIQUE (user_id,operation_type,client_key), PROCESSING/COMPLETED, journal FK and cached HTTP response. Same normalized payload replay yields original receipt and `replayed:true`, even after further balance changes; conflict returns 409, still processing 409, failed owner transaction rolls back and allows retry. This is distinct from Part04 `customer_account_opening_idempotency` (optional opening key) and Part06 transfer receipt lookup. Deposit/withdraw have no GET receipt-by-key. Web same-key manual uncertain retry is functional in a current page but not durable across reload; user can start a different logical operation without reconciling the uncertain one, thus narrower TXN-010 L3.

**Stored versus derived balances and reconciliation:** Authoritative journals/postings retain accounting facts; `accounts.balance` is a materialized field the UI and financial overdraft check read under lock. `JournalPostingRepository.calculateDerivedLiabilityBalance` sums `+CREDIT -DEBIT` for a given customer liability ledger. `LedgerReconciliationServiceImpl.reconcileAll()/reconcileAccount(UUID)` compares stored versus derived scale4 balances, returns discrepancy objects and logs differences, read-only; no controller, scheduler or operation to correct differences was found. Core account closure (ACC-012) separately requires both stored and derived zero. There is no separate available, pending or reserved balance model found in these financial workflows.

**History and lifecycle:** Runtime journal statuses are **POSTED only**, and errors roll back rather than persisting a FAILED journal. Transactions projection has type DEPOSIT, WITHDRAWAL, TRANSFER_IN, TRANSFER_OUT but no independent status. For a successful DEPOSIT/WITHDRAWAL, LegacyTransactionProjectionServiceImpl creates one `transactions` row including positive magnitude, account-specific balanceAfter, journalEntryId, createdAt; TRANSFER produces source+destination rows sharing journal; reversal creates inverse-type projection rows rather than mutating original. V4 unique (journal_entry_id,account_id) prevents duplicate per-account per-journal. Account history GET /api/v1/accounts/{uuid}/transactions is owner-or-staff protected, sorted newest-first, returns TransactionResponseDTO without journal ID/reference, supports closed accounts. HistoryView web displays and client-side CSV exports rows; account history is **legacy transaction projection**, not journal queries. No history pagination/search/date ranges or full bank statement. Generic `GET /transactions`, `GET /transactions/my`, and `GET /transactions/{id}` return literal placeholder strings despite method role/owner guards. Web/Admin journal reverse client helper has no connected Admin UI; actual ADMIN POST reversal API creates compensating `REVERSAL` journal; complete functionality reserved Part12.

**Cutover:** [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql) defines ledger/claim/account currency schema. [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql) fails fast for negative balances, non-LKR, prior marker or partial ledger; creates system vault and separate system opening-balance asset, customer liabilities and balanced per-account positive `OPENING_BALANCE` journals in an explicit rollback-protected SQL transaction without altering legacy Account.balance or transactions. [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__legacy_transaction_projection.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__legacy_transaction_projection.sql) links journal IDs to legacy history via nullable FK and unique journal/account, and raises history precision to DECIMAL(19,4). Actual production cutover results were not observed.

**Verification:** [web/playwright.config.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/playwright.config.ts) runs web Playwright with [web/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs) (not actual Spring/MySQL); [web/e2e/financial-retry.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/financial-retry.spec.ts) tests timeout/same-key UI contract against mock core, web Vitest tests mock network. Spring [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialTransactionApiIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialTransactionApiIntegrationTest.java), [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialWorkflowIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialWorkflowIntegrationTest.java), [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/PostingEngineIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/PostingEngineIntegrationTest.java), [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/LegacyTransactionProjectionIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/LegacyTransactionProjectionIntegrationTest.java), [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/CoreIdempotencyCoordinatorIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/CoreIdempotencyCoordinatorIntegrationTest.java), [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/retry/RealMysqlDeadlockRetryIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/retry/RealMysqlDeadlockRetryIntegrationTest.java) and reversal tests are database-oriented integration-test *source*; no run outputs were independently inspected or tests performed. Android ApiContractTest does not verify native banking payment execution. No attributable actual integrated F1–F8 Part 05 acceptance report was found in the GitHub trees. All individual ratings are **Source-confirmed**, none L5; no source-code modifications or financial transactions.

#### TXN-001 — Customer web deposit and posted deposit receipt

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER (web); internal Core deposit service.
- **User objective:** Credit an owned active LKR account through a posted internal-vault deposit.
- **Workflow boundary:** Credit an owned active LKR account through a posted internal-vault deposit; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Customer route /customer/transactions/deposit renders FinancialPage/FinancialForm; GET /api/v1/accounts/my chooses own account; positive decimal-text amount is validated; submit sends POST /api/v1/transactions/deposit body {accountId,amount:string} and required Idempotency-Key through CSRF-protected BFF. TransactionController CUSTOMER -> FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.deposit -> owner/ACTIVE/LKR/liability/vault checks -> coordinator -> PostingEngine: DR SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR ASSET, CR customer LIABILITY -> Account.balance increases; journal/postings and one DEPOSIT history projection persist. HTTP 200 DepositReceipt includes journalEntryId, journalReference, amount, currency, resultingBalance, postedAt,replayed; web shows journal reference then GET fresh account/history.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/app/(customer)/customer/transactions/deposit/page.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/(customer)/customer/transactions/deposit/page.tsx); [web/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx) Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/transactions.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/transactions.ts); [web/lib/api/http.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/http.ts); [web/app/api/v1/[...path]/route.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/api/v1/%5B...path%5D/route.ts). Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/DepositReceipt.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/DepositReceipt.java)
- **Database / ledger:** Account.balance DECIMAL(19,4) and customer LedgerAccount; journal_entries one POSTED DEPOSIT, two journal_postings, one transactions projection; coordinator claim (user,DEPOSIT,key) in same DB unit of work.
- **External dependencies:** Internal Spring + MySQL/ledger where described; no source-confirmed external cash settlement, third-party processor, or FastAPI dependency for these financial workflows.
- **Authorization:** @PreAuthorize CUSTOMER; principal User UUID mapped through Customer and owned account; ACTIVE/LKR/status and ledger mapping rechecked by Core.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Positive amount yields posted journal + increased stored balance and receipt. Zero, negative, >2 digits, missing key, wrong owner, frozen/closed, unsupported currency or absent vault reject without a successful journal; web exposes API error and manual same-key uncertain retry.
- **Existing automated tests:** [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialTransactionApiIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialTransactionApiIntegrationTest.java); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialWorkflowIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/FinancialWorkflowIntegrationTest.java); [web/tests/financial-form.test.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/tests/financial-form.test.tsx); [web/e2e/financial-retry.spec.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/e2e/financial-retry.spec.ts). Tests were read as source, not executed. SpringBootTest and MockMvc source tests are not passing-run evidence; browser Playwright configured against mock bank-core.
- **Available live verification:** No independently accessible attributable real-stack Part 05 acceptance artifact in the inspected repository source; no tests or transactions executed.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented**, defined for this feature's stated actual scope and evidence only.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** Positive amount yields posted journal + increased stored balance and receipt. Zero, negative, >2 digits, missing key, wrong owner, frozen/closed, unsupported currency or absent vault reject without a successful journal; web exposes API error and manual same-key uncertain retry.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** The debit is an internal system-vault accounting entry, not independently verified external cash receipt/settlement. No pre-submit review screen, external funding source or receipt persistence UI after reload.
- **Source references:** Pinned links above; relevant authoritative migrations: [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql) and [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__legacy_transaction_projection.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__legacy_transaction_projection.sql). Core main `9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3`, web main `012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27`, Android main `9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38`.
- **Future implementation opportunities:** Define source-of-funds/cash settlement integration and optional explicit confirmation if desired.


#### TXN-002 — Customer web withdrawal and no-overdraft enforcement

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER (web).
- **User objective:** Debit an owned account without permitting a negative balance.
- **Workflow boundary:** Debit an owned account without permitting a negative balance; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Customer route /customer/transactions/withdraw uses the shared FinancialForm, selects account from GET /accounts/my, validates positive amount, sends POST /api/v1/transactions/withdraw body {accountId,amount:string} plus Idempotency-Key through BFF. Controller CUSTOMER -> FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.withdraw -> owner/ACTIVE/LKR + liability/vault -> PostingEngine DR customer LIABILITY and CR system vault ASSET; under account lock computes stored balance minus amount, rejects negative with ERR_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS, persists POSTED WITHDRAWAL journal, two postings, one withdrawal projection; HTTP 200 WithdrawalReceipt with reference/resultingBalance/replayed, web refreshes account and history.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/app/(customer)/customer/transactions/withdraw/page.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/app/(customer)/customer/transactions/withdraw/page.tsx); [web/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx) Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).

FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.loadOwnedActiveLkrAccount locks/loads Account by account UUID, checks Account.status ACTIVE, account.currency LKR, resolves Customer by User.userId, requires Customer.customerId == Account.customer.customerId, validates customer LIABILITY LedgerAccount mapping ACTIVE/LKR and vault ASSET ACTIVE/LKR. PostingEngine locks/refreshed account and rejects non-ACTIVE, inconsistent currency, corrupt ledger and insufficient funds again. Transfers require source owner and distinct source/destination; destination need not belong to source user; full Pay UX Part 06.

#### TXN-004 — Customer liability and system-vault ledger-account architecture

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.

Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** V2 creates ledger_accounts with UUID, exactly one customer_account_id OR system_code identity, ASSET/LIABILITY/EQUITY class, currency, ACTIVE/FROZEN/CLOSED ledger status. V3 cutover seeds SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR and SYSTEM_OPENING_BALANCE:LKR system ASSET rows, and one customer LIABILITY ledger row per legacy Account; new AccountServiceImpl creates one customer liability mapping per account. FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl resolves customer liability by customerAccountId, and active system vault by code, validating class, status and LKR. Account UUID and LedgerAccount UUID are distinct; Account.balance stored separately.

#### TXN-005 — Balanced immutable journal and posting engine

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Internal financial infrastructure for customer writes and admin reversals.
- **User objective:** Produce balanced auditable accounting events from supported financial commands.
- **Workflow boundary:** Produce balanced auditable accounting events from supported financial commands; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** PostingEngineImpl.post accepts PostingCommand with currency, journal type, actor, channel, and >=2 PostingInstructions; validates each MonetaryAmount currency, ledger identity/ACTIVE/status and sums debit and credit magnitudes at scale 4, rejects ERR_UNBALANCED_JOURNAL and >DECIMAL(19,4) overflow. Finds affected Customer Accounts, locks in canonical UUID order, applies liability debit/credit deltas, then writes immutable JournalEntry (entryId, unique reference, DEPOSIT/WITHDRAWAL/TRANSFER, POSTED status, actor/channel/time) and JournalPostings (posting IDs, ledger IDs, ordered 0..n seq, debit/credit direction); legacy transaction projections synchronous. One @Transactional boundary. Reversal uses distinct postReversal, OPENING_BALANCE reserved V3 migration.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature). Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).. Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.

- **Database / ledger:** V2 journal_entries FK user and reversal parent, UNIQUE entry_reference and reversal_of; journal_postings FK to journal+ledger, UNIQUE(entry_id,seq), positive amounts DECIMAL(19,4), posting direction and status CHECK. @Immutable JPA entities, not standalone SQL read API.
- **External dependencies:** Internal Spring + MySQL/ledger where described; no source-confirmed external cash settlement, third-party processor, or FastAPI dependency for these financial workflows.
- **Authorization:** Only trusted app workflows call engine; ledger post itself has no independent CUSTOMER HTTP endpoint.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Equal debits/credits create an authoritative POSTED journal, posting legs and Account.balance effects; mismatches or DB failure roll back entire transaction.

- **Supported behavior:** Equal debits/credits create an authoritative POSTED journal, posting legs and Account.balance effects; mismatches or DB failure roll back entire transaction.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** DB CHECKs enforce individual leg structure, but aggregate debit=credit is a PostingEngine application invariant, not a cross-row SQL CHECK; journal read-by-id API is not exposed for customer/staff.

#### TXN-006 — Atomic balance updates, locked posting and overdraft prevention

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Internal finance + CUSTOMER transactions + staff closure dependency.
- **User objective:** Prevent lost balance updates, partial postings and overdrafts under concurrent commands.
- **Workflow boundary:** Prevent lost balance updates, partial postings and overdrafts under concurrent commands; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** PostingEngineImpl @Transactional gathers affected customer IDs from ledger legs, sorts UUIDs, obtains AccountRepository.findByIdForUpdate PESSIMISTIC_WRITE, EntityManager.refresh under lock, validates ACTIVE and currency, aggregates account deltas and only applies if updated balance >=0 (ERR_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS otherwise), updates Account.balance and timestamps. Saves Account, journal, leg rows, synchronous projection in same DB transaction and entityManager.flush; failure rolls back. Transfer and reversal use canonical account ordering; closeAccount uses same row lock to rule out concurrent posts to closed account. Source tests cover simultaneous withdrawals, contrary-direction transfers, lock contention, journal insert failure and projection failure.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx) Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).. Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/repository/AccountRepository.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/repository/AccountRepository.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/application/impl/AccountServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/LegacyTransactionProjectionServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/LegacyTransactionProjectionServiceImpl.java)

#### TXN-007 — Strict decimal-text monetary contract and LKR precision

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER web + internal ledger.
- **User objective:** Preserve financial amounts through UI, API, Java arithmetic and SQL.
- **Workflow boundary:** Preserve financial amounts through UI, API, Java arithmetic and SQL; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Web FinancialForm stores raw decimal text; lib/finance/money.ts accepts positive plain decimal with <=2 fractional digits, rejects scientific, symbols, negatives, zero; transactionsApi sends JSON string amount (not JS Number) via BFF. Spring DepositRequest/WithdrawalRequest/TransferRequest use StrictDecimalTextDeserializer rejecting JSON numeric token; FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl calls MonetaryAmount.fromCustomerInput with CurrencyCode.LKR, BigDecimal plain regex, scale <=2, positive, normalizes to scale4 with UNNECESSARY (no rounding). PostingEngine total maximum 999999999999999.9999, Account.balance and SQL journal/posting/transactions DECIMAL(19,4). JSON account/receipt numeric BigDecimal parsed as decimal strings by web HTTP layer; web display preserves server string. CurrencyCode validates ISO-4217 formatting and registry, but customer finance workflows hardcode LKR.

#### TXN-008 — Persisted financial-operation idempotency and receipt replay

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER financial operations, internal coordinator.
- **User objective:** Execute a keyed deposit/withdrawal/transfer once and replay the original journal receipt.
- **Workflow boundary:** Execute a keyed deposit/withdrawal/transfer once and replay the original journal receipt; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** TransactionController deposit/withdraw/transfer calls requireKey() even though @RequestHeader required=false; absent/invalid key fails validation (400). Workflow validates IdempotencyKey (ASCII <=128) and canonical SHA256 fingerprint by operation/account(s)/LKR/normalized amount. CoreIdempotencyCoordinator @Transactional uses MySQL INSERT IGNORE claim with UNIQUE (user_id,operation_type,client_key) state PROCESSING then locks existing claim for replay; while PROCESSING returns ERR_IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS, same fingerprint COMPLETED replays stored HTTP 200 payload and journal entry ID with replayed=true, changed payload returns ERR_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT; race waits on committed claim or retries after owner rollback. POST and balanced ledger/legacy projection plus persisted COMPLETED are a single physical transaction; FK claim to journal. Transfer receipt read-only GET exists only for transfer, full Part06.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/lib/finance/idempotency.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/finance/idempotency.ts); [web/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx) Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/transactions.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/transactions.ts). Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/application/CoreIdempotencyCoordinator.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/application/CoreIdempotencyCoordinator.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/domain/CoreRequestFingerprint.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/domain/CoreRequestFingerprint.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/domain/repository/CoreTransactionIdempotencyRepository.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/domain/repository/CoreTransactionIdempotencyRepository.java)
- **Database / ledger:** core_transaction_idempotency V2: PRIMARY id, UNIQUE(user_id,operation_type,client_key), entry_id FK journal, state PROCESSING/COMPLETED and cached status/payload. Separate from customer_account_opening_idempotency in V5.
- **External dependencies:** Internal Spring + MySQL/ledger where described; no source-confirmed external cash settlement, third-party processor, or FastAPI dependency for these financial workflows.

Cross-user or cross-operation same text key independent.

#### TXN-009 — Bounded fresh-transaction deadlock retry for financial commands

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Internal financial service, indirectly customer/admin endpoints.
- **User objective:** Retry safe MySQL deadlock rollbacks without duplicating financial effects.
- **Workflow boundary:** Retry safe MySQL deadlock rollbacks without duplicating financial effects; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** FinancialWorkflowServiceImpl deposit/withdraw/transfer delegates complete coordinator+posting action to BoundedFinancialTransactionRetryExecutor, which uses FinancialTransactionAttempt @Transactional(REQUIRES_NEW) for each attempt, up to 3 attempts with 25ms/50ms backoff. TransientTransactionFailureClassifier retries MySQL deadlock 1213 or SQLSTATE 40001, explicitly excludes lock-wait timeout 1205 even if wrapped. ReversalWorkflow also uses executor. Retry exhaust maps to 503 ERR_TRANSACTION_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE; no partial ledger rows if attempt rolled back; idempotency claim is recreated/reused safely.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx) Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).. Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.

#### TXN-010 — Web deposit/withdraw uncertain-response retry experience

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** CUSTOMER web.
- **User objective:** Recover an ambiguous deposit/withdraw POST without causing a second keyed posting.
- **Workflow boundary:** Recover an ambiguous deposit/withdraw POST without causing a second keyed posting; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** FinancialForm creates logical operation key via crypto.randomUUID, locks fields while pending, stores original payload and key in React state, blocks duplicate clicks. On BFF timeout 504, unavailable 502, retryable transport or 409 ERR_IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS it marks uncertain and displays 'may already have succeeded'; manual 'Retry same transaction' reuses identical key/payload and Core replays journal when committed. Confirmed success fetches fresh GET /accounts/{uuid} and /accounts/{uuid}/transactions. Definitive 422 insufficient funds etc unlocks correction. User can intentionally 'Start a different transaction' from uncertain state even without checking history; state is not persisted across reload/navigation; no deposit/withdraw GET receipt-by-key endpoint.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/FinancialForm.tsx); [web/lib/finance/idempotency.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/finance/idempotency.ts) Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [web/lib/api/transactions.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/transactions.ts); [web/lib/api/errors.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/api/errors.ts); [web/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/lib/server/banking-upstream.ts). Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/application/CoreIdempotencyCoordinator.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/idempotency/application/CoreIdempotencyCoordinator.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java)

- **Authorization:** Same authenticated Customer/user scope for replay; BFF CSRF for POST, Core role and owner rules remain.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Manual same-key retry is economically idempotent, receipt and fresh balance presented if request succeeds; uncertain outcome warning before user decides next action.

#### TXN-011 — Synchronous ledger-to-legacy transaction history projection

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Internal posting path; account owner history reads.
- **User objective:** Expose immutable posted journal effects as account transaction-history rows.
- **Workflow boundary:** Expose immutable posted journal effects as account transaction-history rows; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** PostingEngineImpl after journal and posting legs synchronously calls LegacyTransactionProjectionServiceImpl with journal + net customer balance deltas and locked Accounts. For DEPOSIT creates 1 Transaction(type DEPOSIT, positive amount, balanceAfter, journalEntryId); for WITHDRAWAL 1 WITHDRAWAL, amount positive; TRANSFER creates 2 rows TRANSFER_OUT/TRANSFER_IN sharing one journalEntryId; reversal inserts compensating rows reflecting inverse signs/transaction types. Projection uses @Transactional(MANDATORY), repository save and V4 UNIQUE(journal_entry_id,account_id); failure of second row/DB trigger rolls back account balances/journal/previous projection. Pre-ledger historic rows may have null journal_entry_id and remain queryable.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/HistoryView.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/HistoryView.tsx) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/AccountTransactionsScreen.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/AccountTransactionsScreen.kt)
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).. Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/LegacyTransactionProjectionServiceImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/LegacyTransactionProjectionServiceImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/domain/Transaction.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/domain/Transaction.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java)
- **Database / ledger:** transactions.transaction_id UUID, account_id FK, type enum DEPOSIT/WITHDRAWAL/TRANSFER_IN/TRANSFER_OUT, amount+balance_after DECIMAL(19,4), created_at, nullable journal_entry_id FK; V4 unique journal+account.
- **External dependencies:** Internal Spring + MySQL/ledger where described; no source-confirmed external cash settlement, third-party processor, or FastAPI dependency for these financial workflows.
- **Authorization:** Synchronous service no external endpoint; read exposure via owner/staff account history guard.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Journal accepted iff corresponding projection writes succeed; list includes new and historic rows newest first; two-sided transfer has distinct customer projections.
- **Existing automated tests:** [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/LegacyTransactionProjectionIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/LegacyTransactionProjectionIntegrationTest.java); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/PostingEngineIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/PostingEngineIntegrationTest.java). Tests were read as source, not executed. SpringBootTest and MockMvc source tests are not passing-run evidence; browser Playwright configured against mock bank-core.

- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** Legacy projection is not a complete journal read API or separate transaction status object; transaction list DTO does not surface journal reference/entryId. Reversal rows use inverse economic transaction type, not explicit REVERSED flag.

#### TXN-013 — Staff account-scoped transaction history visibility

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** ADMIN, TELLER, MANAGER staff readers.
- **User objective:** Access history for a staff-selected customer account.
- **Workflow boundary:** Access history for a staff-selected customer account; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Staff /staff/accounts listing -> /staff/accounts/{accountId} -> /staff/accounts/{accountId}/transactions uses HistoryView, GET Account and GET /api/v1/accounts/{uuid}/transactions through BFF.
