#### TXN-014 — Read-only stored-vs-ledger balance reconciliation service

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Backend internal service caller; no mapped HTTP/UI.
- **User objective:** Detect drift between Account.balance and liability journal-derived balance.
- **Workflow boundary:** Detect drift between Account.balance and liability journal-derived balance; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** LedgerReconciliationServiceImpl.reconcileAll() reads all Account rows, reconcileAccount(UUID) reads single; looks up customer Liability LedgerAccount by customerAccountId, calculates journalPostingRepository.calculateDerivedLiabilityBalance as SUM(CREDIT amount - DEBIT amount), compares scale4 Account.balance and derived balance, emits LedgerReconciliationResult with totals/count and per-account discrepancy account UUID/number, materialized, derived, difference; logs discrepancies. No mutation or repair. Account list/history GETs use stored Account.balance, not this calculation; account closure invokes derived balance separately under lock. No REST reconciliation controller, scheduler, dashboard, alert or repair workflow in inspected source.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature). Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).. Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.

#### TXN-015 — Admin-only journal reversal and compensating postings API

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** ADMIN direct API; no connected Admin web UI confirmed.
- **User objective:** Correct a posted eligible journal without editing original financial history.
- **Workflow boundary:** Correct a posted eligible journal without editing original financial history; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** TransactionController POST /api/v1/transactions/{journalEntryId}/reversal @PreAuthorize ADMIN with ReversalRequest {reason:not blank}, authenticates actor. ReversalWorkflowImpl -> bounded fresh retry -> PostingEngineImpl.postReversal, locks original POSTED journal row, disallows reverse of REVERSAL/OPENING_BALANCE, validates original shape/class/currency/postings and active accounts, queries existing reversal_of natural unique identity, inserts new REVERSAL journal and opposite debit/credit postings, applies inverse customer Account.balance and creates inverse legacy Transaction projection. Repeat original returns natural replay receipt with reversalJournalEntryId, original id, amount/currency, resulting balances, postedAt. If original funds already spent and reversal would overdraw, rejects atomically. No unambiguous journal-list/read API or connected Admin reversal form; web transactionsApi.reverse client helper is not a workflow.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature). Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).

- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/TransactionController.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/ReversalWorkflowImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/application/impl/ReversalWorkflowImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/dto/ReversalResponse.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/interfaces/dto/ReversalResponse.java)
- **Database / ledger:** journal_entries REVERSAL links reversal_of_entry_id UNIQUE FK to original; original POSTED journal unmodified; new postings/history in single transaction; no mutable journal status REVERSED.

#### TXN-018 — Android real per-account transaction history, distinct from Home demo

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Android CUSTOMER account owner.
- **User objective:** Read true Core account history from My Accounts on phone.
- **Workflow boundary:** Read true Core account history from My Accounts on phone; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** AppNavHost Accounts My -> account history route; AccountTransactionsRoute/ViewModel uses AccountRepository.getAccountTransactions(accountId) -> AccountsApi Retrofit GET /api/v1/accounts/{id}/transactions with bearer -> Spring AccountController staff-or-owner authorization -> TransactionQueryServiceImpl -> TransactionRepository sorted history -> Android maps transactionId/accountId/type/amount/balanceAfter/description/createdAt to domain; AccountTransactionsScreen shows activity list with loading skeleton, empty and error, Refresh and account number hide/reveal. This screen fetches actual Core transaction projections, unlike Dashboard Home's hardcoded recent items (DASH-009). Android TransactionNet and domain use Double for amount/balanceAfter, UI formatAmount signs all nonnegative amounts '+', including withdrawal and transfer-out because Core returns positive magnitudes. Date parsing uses UTC interpretation.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: [web/components/banking/HistoryView.tsx](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-web/blob/012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27/components/banking/HistoryView.tsx) Android: [app/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/AccountTransactionsRoute.kt](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-app/blob/9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38/app/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/mobile/features/accounts/interfaces/ui/AccountTransactionsRoute.kt); #### TXN-019 — Ledger migration, balance cutover and legacy-history compatibility

- **Review part:** 05 — banking transactions and double-entry ledger.
- **User roles:** Flyway database bootstrap/migration infrastructure.
- **User objective:** Introduce double-entry accounting without silently losing existing stored balances/history.
- **Workflow boundary:** Introduce double-entry accounting without silently losing existing stored balances/history; scoped to the concrete client or internal operation named here. Account opening/closure scored in Part 04, complete Pay Part 06, staff workspace Part 08, full Admin reversal Part 12 and full Android Part 14 are not rescored.
- **Complete workflow steps:** Flyway V1 baseline stores Account and legacy transactions. V2 adds accounts.currency, ledger_accounts, journal_entries, journal_postings and core_transaction_idempotency with constraints. V3 prechecks no negative balances, only LKR, no ledger cutover marker/preexisting ledger rows; then one SQL transaction with exception rollback persists ledger_cutover_at marker, seeds SYSTEM_VAULT_CASH:LKR and SYSTEM_OPENING_BALANCE:LKR, creates a customer LIABILITY ledger account for each existing Account, and for preexisting positive balances writes POSTED OPENING_BALANCE journal with debit SYSTEM_OPENING_BALANCE asset and credit customer LIABILITY. Legacy Account.balance and transactions remain unchanged. V4 adds journalEntryId nullable FK and UNIQUE(journal_entry_id,account_id), upgrades legacy transaction decimals to scale4. Runtime PostingEngine rejects OPENING_BALANCE (migration-only) and reversals use dedicated method. Source migration tests cover preconditions, rollback/second startup and preserved balances.
- **Frontend / Android:** Web: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature). Android: Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** Not applicable (no independent client/transport for this scoped feature).. Web calls use CSRF-aware authenticated Next BFF forwarding; Android Core reads use direct Retrofit bearer.
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/domain/JournalEntryType.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/domain/JournalEntryType.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/ledger/application/impl/PostingEngineImpl.java); [core/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/Account.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/accounts/domain/Account.java)
- **Database / ledger:** V1/V2/V3/V4 migrations; V3 is idempotent-by-precondition not a recurring posting workflow; V2 journal status only POSTED and ledger account separate from Account.
- **External dependencies:** Internal Spring + MySQL/ledger where described; no source-confirmed external cash settlement, third-party processor, or FastAPI dependency for these financial workflows.
- **Authorization:** Migration run by configured database/Flyway bootstrapping, not a customer/staff operation.
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** Existing materialized Account balances are represented by balanced cutover journals and historical transaction rows stay queryable.
- **Existing automated tests:** [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/CoreLedgerMigrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/CoreLedgerMigrationTest.java); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/MigrationAuthorityTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/MigrationAuthorityTest.java); [core/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/LegacyTransactionProjectionIntegrationTest.java](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/test/java/com/bankingsystem/core/features/transactions/LegacyTransactionProjectionIntegrationTest.java). Tests were read as source, not executed. SpringBootTest and MockMvc source tests are not passing-run evidence; browser Playwright configured against mock bank-core.
- **Available live verification:** No independently accessible attributable real-stack Part 05 acceptance artifact in the inspected repository source; no tests or transactions executed.
- **Completeness level:** **Level 4 — Functionally implemented**, defined for this feature's stated actual scope and evidence only.
- **Independent status:** None.
- **Evidence confidence:** **Source-confirmed**.
- **Supported behavior:** Existing materialized Account balances are represented by balanced cutover journals and historical transaction rows stay queryable.
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** No evidence of an operational production migration run, balances reconciled post-deployment or scheduler running; historic transaction rows can lack journal IDs. No automated interest/fees, FX or external settlement capability established.
- **Source references:** Pinned links above; relevant authoritative migrations: [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__core_ledger.sql), [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__opening_balance_migration.sql) and [core/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__legacy_transaction_projection.sql](https://github.com/gimesha-adikari/bank-core/blob/9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3/src/main/resources/db/migration/V4__legacy_transaction_projection.sql). Core main `9f58e595a653db9d8aa59b70a35af8e2b0e725d3`, web main `012fbc6ff2687d126c67c85ce54cfc8d16f79f27`, Android main `9d98ba24cb754b8295bd58770cd502438d3b7b38`.
- **Future implementation opportunities:** Operational migration reconciliation and archival controls as part of platform reliability review.

## 10. Standard feature record template

Copy this structure into the appropriate part when a feature is actually reviewed; replace placeholders only with observed evidence:

```md
#### [FEATURE-ID] — [Feature name]

- **Review part:** [00–16]
- **User roles:** [roles and permissions]
- **User objective:** [reason this feature is used]
- **Workflow boundary:** [entry condition through observable outcome]
- **Complete workflow steps:** [ordered stages and branch conditions]
- **Frontend / Android:** [routes/screens/components/action links, or N/A]
- **Next.js BFF / API integration:** [client, endpoint, method, payload, response, error handling, or N/A]
- **Spring Boot implementation:** [controllers/services/validation, or N/A]
- **Database / ledger:** [entities, tables, migrations, journal effects, or N/A]
- **External dependencies:** [FastAPI, provider, mail, etc., or N/A]
- **Authorization:** [authn/authz, role/ownership checks]
- **Success, failure and recovery behavior:** [actual user-visible states]
- **Existing automated tests:** [file paths, scope, mock boundaries]
- **Available live verification:** [dated evidence, environment and scope, or none]
- **Completeness level:** [0–5, or unscored when insufficient evidence]
- **Independent status:** [status, if applicable]
- **Evidence confidence:** [one of the five evidence labels]
- **Supported behavior:** [precisely what works from inspected evidence]
- **Unsupported / incomplete behavior:** [precise missing portions]
- **Source references:** [commit-pinned repo links and file paths]
- **Future implementation opportunities:** [possible follow-ups, not defects]
```

## 13. Known evidence limitations

- Part 00 inspected GitHub repository metadata, the default-branch refs, three known commits and root-level directories **only**.
- No frontend-to-backend workflow tracing, test inspection, API verification or live-stack acceptance was performed during Part 00.
- Matching the named local commits to GitHub main does not establish the absence of subsequent unpublished changes.
- No expected local commit was provided for bank-app, so local-vs-GitHub parity for the Android app is unknown.
- Future parts should provide source links pinned to inspected commits, distinguish documentation from independent verification, and disclose relevant access or version gaps.

- Part 01 inspected GitHub source only at the four pinned SHAs above, including web BFF, Spring security/services/entities, Android auth/storage/lock, and FastAPI internal authorization. No code was executed, no credentials tested, and no commits modified application sources.
- The committed Playwright tests run a mock core via `e2e/mock-bank-core.mjs`; they do not establish a real Spring/MySQL/Mailpit workflow. The Spring integration and unit tests were read as code, not executed in this review.
- No historical F1–F8 real-stack reports were found as readable evidence within the four GitHub trees. Their contents may exist outside these repositories; none were used to claim live verification.
- Android device/emulator runtime and email SMTP delivery were not observed. Hence all Part 01 evidence confidence is Source-confirmed and no Level 5 is asserted.
- Config-dependent email-change link destination and cross-platform difference in password-change sign-out are identified as completeness boundaries, not established live defects.
- Features not found in inspected source (MFA, social login, device-session management, etc.) are catalogued only as unsupported possibilities, not falsely scored as implemented.
- Part 02 inspected source only at pinned GitHub SHAs; no application source changed, and no tests, device/emulator runs, ML inference, real document processing, SMTP, or database mutations were performed.
- No attributable historical F1–F8 actual integrated-stack execution reports or verifiable outputs were accessible in the reviewed four GitHub repository trees; web Playwright uses mock bank-core.
- KycMlContractIT is environment-gated integration test source, not independently confirmed successful execution. Optional ONNX model weights are not tracked in reviewed bank-service source; local/external model snapshots were not available for verification.
- Part 02 found explicit source limitations in NEEDS_MORE_INFO customer correction, Android placeholder face/liveness indicators, mobile submission recovery and upload-draft persistence.
- Part 02 confirms latest approved KYC and separately saved Customer record are both required for self-service account opening; this review does not certify account-opening/ledger, Admin-workspace completeness, staff lifecycle or whole Android platform.

- Part 03 rechecked four GitHub main baselines and traced web dashboard, profile, BFF, Spring User/Customer/Account APIs and Android Home/Profile/My Accounts navigation and local settings. bank-service/FastAPI does not participate in dashboard or profile reads/updates.
- Web dashboard gets the Customer record for existence, account list for stored per-account status/balance and conditional latest KYC for no-account eligibility. It has no aggregated balance or real recent activity feed. Navigation links are not destination workflow verification.
- Web /customer/profile only reads User; banking Customer fields are maintained through /customer/onboarding with approved latest KYC. General backend User contact edits are available via Android, not a general web User editor; overlapping fields need not synchronize.
- Android Home uses real account-backed single balance but does not surface errors, uses dollar sign rather than LKR, shows fixed recent transactions and inert quick actions/Notifications; Android My Accounts and profile support real API reads/edits plus a device-local default account.
- Tests inspected but not run. Web Playwright uses mock bank-core; Android ApiContractTest covers serialization only; no attributable real integrated-stack Part 03 evidence accessible. Every Part 03 record Source-confirmed; no Level 5.
- Account lifecycle (04), transactions and ledger (05), Internal Pay (06), staff Customer management (07), and full Android app (14) remain not reviewed beyond Part 03 integration boundaries.

- Part 04 checked all four prior GitHub main refs at pinned commits, then source-reviewed bank-web, bank-core and bank-app account lifecycle. No Spring account-lifecycle call to bank-service/FastAPI exists, so no artificial FastAPI dependency.
- Core offers SAVINGS/CHECKING/FIXED_DEPOSIT with LKR opening minimums 1000/0/5000; Branch is persisted and required, but branch list ignores pagination, lacks status and branch admin mutations are stubs. No demonstrated interest/term/fee product lifecycle.
- Customer self-service POST /accounts enforces role, linked banking Customer and latest APPROVED KYC; Teller POST /accounts/{userUuid} uses a different role and bypasses latest KYC. Neither opening path checks Customer.status ACTIVE or account count.
- Self-service web opening holds page-memory Idempotency-Key and decimal string, Core persists user-scoped idempotency claim and account+ledger/funding under one transaction. Claims replay successful response; no-key direct API, Teller and Android lack this durable contract. Positive opening deposit posts from system vault to Customer liability; zero Checking does not post journal/transaction.
- Account UUID, 10-digit account number, User UUID, Customer UUID and integer Branch ID are distinct. AccountResponseDTO omits branch/currency/owner; customer /accounts/my lists all statuses and by-ID reads enforce ownership; staff /accounts directory has no pagination.
- Staff PUT changes type/branch (reuses opening DTO and ignores initialDeposit); staff-only DELETE checks ACTIVE and zero Account.balance and ledger-derived balance under a posting-compatible row lock, persists CLOSED and retains history. changeAccountStatus for FROZEN/ACTIVE exists only as service method without a route; no customer closure.
- Android opening has type and branch input but Double amount serialization, no Idempotency-Key/recovery and unwired KYC banner. Dashboard account display and My Accounts default choice remain Part 03; deeper ledger Part 05 and staff Part 08 and Android Part 14 remain NOT REVIEWED.
- Part 04 inspected existing mock-backed web Playwright, Vitest and Spring/Android test source without running tests; no attributable live real-stack acceptance was available; all confidence Source-confirmed, no Level 5.

- Part 05 reconfirmed unchanged four GitHub main commits and source-reviewed bank-web, bank-core and bank-app financial paths; no genuine bank-service/FastAPI dependency for banking transaction ledger. No source or database changes, no test execution.
- Web CUSTOMER deposit and withdrawal POST call Spring through CSRF-protected BFF with required idempotency keys; authenticated User-to-Customer Account owner, ACTIVE/LKR, ACTIVE liability ledger, active LKR system vault are independently validated. No external cash or payment settlement proven.
- PostingEngine balances debit/credit in immutable POSTED journals/postings, locks and refreshes customer Account rows in canonical order, prevents overdraft and writes Account.balance plus journal legs and mandatory legacy projection atomically. Transfer uses same infrastructure but whole Pay remains Part 06.
- Web financial amounts remain precise decimal strings, Spring rejects numeric JSON amount and uses BigDecimal up to 2 customer decimals/4 ledger decimals and LKR-only financial operations; Android history uses Double and misleading positive sign for withdrawal magnitude.
- Core financial Idempotency-Key for deposit/withdraw/transfer is required via requireKey, durable user/operation/key scoped claim with normalized fingerprint, cached POSTED journal receipt and original balance replay. Web same-key uncertain retry is in-memory only; no deposit/withdraw GET receipt lookup.
- Read-only reconciliation service compares stored account balance with journal-derived liability balance but no connected HTTP/scheduler/UI. Journal.status POSTED only, legacy Transaction has type but no status; generic transaction list/detail routes return placeholder text.
- Account-scoped customer/staff history is actual sorted legacy projection via GET /accounts/{uuid}/transactions, supports closed accounts and browser CSV; not a journal read API. Admin-only reversal API makes compensating immutable journal, but no connected Admin UI; complete review Part 12.
- Android deposit/withdraw payment UI is preview-only (no money moved); native per-account history is actual authenticated Core read distinct from hardcoded Home recent transactions. Full Android later Part 14.
- V2/V3/V4 ledger migration defines system vault/cutover assets, customer liabilities, balanced opening cutover journal and legacy history FK/precision; actual deployment outcomes not inspected.
- Web Playwright runs mock bank-core; Spring financial/MySQL integration and Android test source were inspected but not run. All 19 TXN records Source-confirmed; 0 L5. Part 06 onward remains NOT REVIEWED.
